## PROJECT OVERVIEW
Figma2Word is a production TypeScript compiler that converts Figma designs and Canvas IR
into high-fidelity Microsoft Word (.docx) and Hancom Hangul (.hwpx) documents.
Phase 23 focused on eliminating text overlap and visual border artifacts in Hancom Viewer.

export * from "./types.js";
export * from "./parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";

================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/types.ts
================================================================================

/**
 * Canvas IR (Intermediate Representation) v1
 * 
 * Represents a document as a set of pages, each containing elements with
 * exact 2D coordinates (x, y, width, height, z-index), styling, typography,
 * and media resources.
 */

// ============================================================================
// 1. Coordinate & Unit Conversions
// ============================================================================

/**
 * Standard unit conversions:
 * Internal unit is PostScript point (pt, 1/72 inch).
 * 
 * - 1 pt = 20 twips (dxa)
 * - 1 pt = 12,700 EMUs (English Metric Units)
 * - 1 pt = 100 HWPUs (Hancom Word Processor Units)
 * - 1 pt = 25.4 / 72 mm ≈ 0.352778 mm
 * - 1 mm = 72 / 25.4 pt ≈ 2.834646 pt
 * - 1 CSS px (96 DPI) = 72 / 96 pt = 0.75 pt
 */

export function ptToTwip(pt: number): number {
  return Math.round(pt * 20);
}

export function twipToPt(twip: number): number {
  return twip / 20;
}

export function ptToEmu(pt: number): number {
  return Math.round(pt * 12700);
}

export function emuToPt(emu: number): number {
  return emu / 12700;
}

export function ptToHwpu(pt: number): number {
  return Math.round(pt * 100);
}

export function hwpuToPt(hwpu: number): number {
  return hwpu / 100;
}

export function ptToMm(pt: number): number {
  return (pt * 25.4) / 72;
}

export function mmToPt(mm: number): number {
  return (mm * 72) / 25.4;
}

export function figmaPxToPt(px: number, dpi: number = 96): number {
  return (px * 72) / dpi;
}

// ============================================================================
// 2. Scale Policy
// ============================================================================

export type ScalePolicy = "css-96dpi" | "fit-a4" | "1:1";

export interface ResolvedScale {
  scaleX: number;
  scaleY: number;
  targetWidthPt: number;
  targetHeightPt: number;
  policy: ScalePolicy;
}

/**
 * Resolves scaling from Figma frame dimensions to output canvas dimensions.
 * Standard A4 portrait in pt: 595.28 x 841.89 pt (210 x 297 mm).
 */
export function resolveScale(
  frameWidthPx: number,
  frameHeightPx: number,
  policy: ScalePolicy = "fit-a4"
): ResolvedScale {
  const A4_WIDTH_PT = 595.28;
  const A4_HEIGHT_PT = 841.89;

  if (policy === "1:1") {
    return {
      scaleX: 1.0,
      scaleY: 1.0,
      targetWidthPt: frameWidthPx,
      targetHeightPt: frameHeightPx,
      policy,
    };
  }

  if (policy === "css-96dpi") {
    const scale = 0.75; // 72 / 96
    return {
      scaleX: scale,
      scaleY: scale,
      targetWidthPt: frameWidthPx * scale,
      targetHeightPt: frameHeightPx * scale,
      policy,
    };
  }

  // policy === 'fit-a4'
  // If frame aspect ratio is close to A4 (0.68 ~ 0.73), scale to fit exact A4 width/height
  const ratio = frameWidthPx / frameHeightPx;
  const isA4Portrait = ratio >= 0.65 && ratio <= 0.75;
  const isA4Landscape = ratio >= 1.35 && ratio <= 1.50;

  if (isA4Portrait) {
    const scaleX = A4_WIDTH_PT / frameWidthPx;
    const scaleY = A4_HEIGHT_PT / frameHeightPx;
    return {
      scaleX,
      scaleY,
      targetWidthPt: A4_WIDTH_PT,
      targetHeightPt: A4_HEIGHT_PT,
      policy,
    };
  }

  if (isA4Landscape) {
    const scaleX = A4_HEIGHT_PT / frameWidthPx;
    const scaleY = A4_WIDTH_PT / frameHeightPx;
    return {
      scaleX,
      scaleY,
      targetWidthPt: A4_HEIGHT_PT,
      targetHeightPt: A4_WIDTH_PT,
      policy,
    };
  }

  // Fallback to standard 96 DPI CSS conversion
  const scale = 0.75;
  return {
    scaleX: scale,
    scaleY: scale,
    targetWidthPt: frameWidthPx * scale,
    targetHeightPt: frameHeightPx * scale,
    policy,
  };
}

export interface CanvasBounds {
  x: number; // pt, relative to page top-left origin (0, 0)
  y: number; // pt, relative to page top-left origin (0, 0)

  height: number; // pt
}

  top?: number;
  right?: number;
  bottom?: number;
  left?: number;
}

  strokeColor?: string; // hex '#RRGGBB'

  colCount: number;

  // Diagnostics and lineage

}

}

    warnings: string[];
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/pipeline/canvasPipeline.ts
================================================================================

import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import { CanvasDocument, ScalePolicy } from "../types.js";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasParser } from "../parser/docxCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { FigmaNode, FigmaFileResponse } from "../../parser/types.js";
import { FigmaSnapshot, isFigmaSnapshot } from "../../pipeline/index.js";
import { FigmaClient, parseFigmaUrl } from "../../api/index.js";
import { resolveImageMapUrls } from "../utils/imageResolver.js";

export interface CanvasPipelineOptions {
  format?: "docx" | "hwpx" | "both";
  scalePolicy?: ScalePolicy;
  selectedFrameIds?: string[];
  imageMap?: Record<string, string>;
  titleOverride?: string;
  outputPath?: string;
  outputDocxPath?: string;
  outputHwpxPath?: string;
}

export interface CanvasPipelineResult {
  canvasDocument: CanvasDocument;
  docxBuffer?: Buffer;
  hwpxBuffer?: Buffer;
  outputDocxPath?: string;
  outputHwpxPath?: string;
  durationMs: number;
  stats: {
    pageCount: number;
    elementCount: number;
    resourceCount: number;
  };
}

export class CanvasPipeline {
  private figmaParser: FigmaCanvasParser;
  private docxParser: DocxCanvasParser;
  private docxRenderer: DocxCanvasRenderer;
  private hwpxRenderer: HwpxCanvasRenderer;

  constructor(options?: { creator?: string }) {
    this.figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
    this.docxParser = new DocxCanvasParser();
    this.docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer({ creator: options?.creator });
    this.hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer({ creator: options?.creator });
  }

  /**
   * Convert Figma AST / Snapshot to Canvas Output (DOCX, HWPX, or Both)
   */
  public async convertFigma(
    input: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse | FigmaSnapshot,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();

    // 1. Unpack FigmaSnapshot if provided
    let rawFigma: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse;
    let mergedImageMap = options?.imageMap || {};

    if (isFigmaSnapshot(input)) {
      rawFigma = input.figmaNode;
      if (input.imageMap) {
        mergedImageMap = { ...input.imageMap, ...mergedImageMap };
      }
    } else {
      rawFigma = input as FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse;
    }

    // 2. Parse Figma into CanvasDocument
    const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({
      imageMap: mergedImageMap,
      selectedFrameIds: options?.selectedFrameIds,
      scalePolicy: options?.scalePolicy || "fit-a4",
      titleOverride: options?.titleOverride,
    });

    const canvasDocument = parser.parse(rawFigma);

    // 3. Render requested formats
    const format = options?.format || "docx";
    let docxBuffer: Buffer | undefined;
    let hwpxBuffer: Buffer | undefined;

    if (format === "docx" || format === "both") {
      docxBuffer = await this.docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDocument);
    }

    if (format === "hwpx" || format === "both") {
      hwpxBuffer = await this.hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDocument);
    }

    // 4. Save to disk if paths provided
    let outDocxPath = options?.outputDocxPath;
    let outHwpxPath = options?.outputHwpxPath;

    if (options?.outputPath) {
      const base = options.outputPath.replace(/\.(docx|hwpx)$/i, "");
      if (format === "docx" || format === "both") {
        outDocxPath = `${base}.docx`;
      }
      if (format === "hwpx" || format === "both") {
        outHwpxPath = `${base}.hwpx`;
      }
    }

    if (docxBuffer && outDocxPath) {
      const dir = path.dirname(outDocxPath);
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outDocxPath, docxBuffer);
    }

    if (hwpxBuffer && outHwpxPath) {
      const dir = path.dirname(outHwpxPath);
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outHwpxPath, hwpxBuffer);
    }

    const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    const elementCount = canvasDocument.pages.reduce(
      (sum, p) => sum + p.elements.length,
      0
    );

    return {
      canvasDocument,
      docxBuffer,
      hwpxBuffer,
      outputDocxPath: outDocxPath,
      outputHwpxPath: outHwpxPath,
      durationMs,
      stats: {
        pageCount: canvasDocument.pages.length,
        elementCount,
        resourceCount: Object.keys(canvasDocument.resources).length,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Convert an existing absolute-canvas DOCX file into HWPX
   */
  public async convertDocxToHwpx(
    docxInput: Buffer | string,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();

    const canvasDocument = await this.docxParser.parse(docxInput);
    const hwpxBuffer = await this.hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(canvasDocument);

    let outHwpxPath = options?.outputHwpxPath || options?.outputPath;
    if (outHwpxPath) {
      if (!outHwpxPath.endsWith(".hwpx")) {
        outHwpxPath = `${outHwpxPath}.hwpx`;
      }
      const dir = path.dirname(outHwpxPath);
      if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      await fs.promises.writeFile(outHwpxPath, hwpxBuffer);
    }

    const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    const elementCount = canvasDocument.pages.reduce(
      (sum, p) => sum + p.elements.length,
      0
    );

    return {
      canvasDocument,
      hwpxBuffer,
      outputHwpxPath: outHwpxPath,
      durationMs,
      stats: {
        pageCount: canvasDocument.pages.length,
        elementCount,
        resourceCount: Object.keys(canvasDocument.resources).length,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Universal file conversion method supporting both .json (Figma snapshot)
   * and .docx (DOCX -> HWPX)
   */
  public async convertFile(
    filePath: string,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const absPath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), filePath);
    if (!fs.existsSync(absPath)) {
      throw new Error(`File not found: ${absPath}`);
    }

    const ext = path.extname(absPath).toLowerCase();

    if (ext === ".docx") {
      return this.convertDocxToHwpx(absPath, options);
    }

    if (ext === ".json") {
      const content = await fs.promises.readFile(absPath, "utf-8");
      const parsed = JSON.parse(content);
      return this.convertFigma(parsed, options);
    }

    throw new Error(`Unsupported file extension for Canvas Engine: ${ext}`);
  }

  /**
   * Fetches a live Figma design via Figma REST API, downloads image fills,
   * and converts to Canvas IR output (DOCX, HWPX, or Both).
   */
  public async convertFigmaUrl(
    figmaUrl: string,
    accessToken: string,
    options?: CanvasPipelineOptions
  ): Promise<CanvasPipelineResult> {
    const urlInfo = parseFigmaUrl(figmaUrl);
    const client = new FigmaClient({ accessToken });

    // 1. Fetch file
    const fileResponse = await client.fetchFile(urlInfo.fileKey);

    // 2. Fetch and resolve remote image fills to Base64
    let imageMap: Record<string, string> = options?.imageMap || {};
    try {
      const rawImageMap = await client.fetchImageFills(urlInfo.fileKey);
      const resolved = await resolveImageMapUrls(rawImageMap, (url) =>
        client.downloadImageAsBase64(url)
      );
      imageMap = { ...imageMap, ...resolved };
    } catch {
      // Continue if image download fails
    }

    let targetNode: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse = fileResponse;
    if (urlInfo.nodeId && fileResponse.document) {
      const findNode = (n: any, id: string): any => {
        if (!n) return undefined;
        if (n.id === id || n.id.replace(/:/g, "-") === id || n.id.replace(/-/g, ":") === id) return n;
        if (n.children) {
          for (const child of n.children) {
            const res = findNode(child, id);
            if (res) return res;
          }
        }
        return undefined;
      };
      const found = findNode(fileResponse.document, urlInfo.nodeId);
      if (found) targetNode = found;
    }

    return this.convertFigma(targetNode, {
      ...options,
      imageMap,
      titleOverride: options?.titleOverride || fileResponse.name,
    });
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/geometry/svgPathParser.ts
================================================================================

/**
 * SVG Path Parser & Geometry Transpiler for Canvas IR, VML, and OWPML (HWPX).
 *
 * Supports SVG path commands:
 * - M / m (Move to)
 * - L / l (Line to)
 * - H / h (Horizontal line to)
 * - V / v (Vertical line to)
 * - C / c (Cubic Bezier)
 * - S / s (Smooth cubic Bezier)
 * - Q / q (Quadratic Bezier)
 * - T / t (Smooth quadratic Bezier)
 * - A / a (Elliptical Arc)
 * - Z / z (Close path)
 */

export interface Point2D {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

export type NormalizedCommandType = "M" | "L" | "C" | "Z";

export interface NormalizedPathCommand {
  type: NormalizedCommandType;
  // For 'M' and 'L': [p1]
  // For 'C': [cp1, cp2, endPoint]
  // For 'Z': []
  points: Point2D[];
}

export interface PathBounds {
  minX: number;
  minY: number;
  maxX: number;
  maxY: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/**
 * Normalize SVG path string to unpack compact arc flags and consecutive signs.
 */
function normalizeSvgPathString(d: string): string {
  // Normalize compact arc flags:
  // e.g. "A 25 25 0 11 150 150" or "A 25 25 0 01 150 150"
  // Match 'A' or 'a', followed by 3 numbers (rx ry rot), then two flags (0 or 1).
  return d.replace(
    /([Aa])((?:\s*[-+]?(?:\d*\.\d+|\d+)(?:[eE][-+]?\d+)?[\s,]*){3})\s*([01])[\s,]*([01])/gi,
    (_m, cmd, first3, f1, f2) => `${cmd}${first3} ${f1} ${f2} `
  );
}

/**
 * Tokenize an SVG path string into command letters and numerical tokens.
 */
function tokenizeSvgPath(d: string): (string | number)[] {
  const normalizedD = normalizeSvgPathString(d);
  const tokens: (string | number)[] = [];
  // Matches command letters, or floats including scientific notation and negative numbers
  const regex = /([a-df-z])|([-+]?(?:\d*\.\d+|\d+)(?:[eE][-+]?\d+)?)/gi;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

  while ((match = regex.exec(normalizedD)) !== null) {
    if (match[1] !== undefined) {
      tokens.push(match[1]);
    } else if (match[2] !== undefined) {
      const num = parseFloat(match[2]);
      if (!isNaN(num)) {
        tokens.push(num);
      }
    }
  }
  return tokens;
}

/**
 * Convert an SVG elliptical arc segment into one or more cubic Bezier curve commands.
 * Based on the W3C SVG 1.1 implementation notes (F.6 Elliptical arc implementation notes).
 */
function arcToCubicBeziers(
  x1: number,
  y1: number,
  rx: number,
  ry: number,
  angleDegrees: number,
  largeArcFlag: boolean,
  sweepFlag: boolean,
  x2: number,
  y2: number
): NormalizedPathCommand[] {
  const commands: NormalizedPathCommand[] = [];

  // If endpoints are identical, arc is degenerate
  if (Math.abs(x1 - x2) < 1e-6 && Math.abs(y1 - y2) < 1e-6) {
    return commands;
  }

  // Ensure radii are positive
  rx = Math.abs(rx);
  ry = Math.abs(ry);
  if (rx < 1e-6 || ry < 1e-6) {
    // Treat as straight line
    commands.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x: x2, y: y2 }] });
    return commands;
  }

  const phi = (angleDegrees * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cosPhi = Math.cos(phi);
  const sinPhi = Math.sin(phi);

  // Step 1: Compute (x1', y1')
  const dx = (x1 - x2) / 2;
  const dy = (y1 - y2) / 2;
  const x1p = cosPhi * dx + sinPhi * dy;
  const y1p = -sinPhi * dx + cosPhi * dy;

  // Correct radii if necessary
  const lambda = (x1p * x1p) / (rx * rx) + (y1p * y1p) / (ry * ry);
  if (lambda > 1) {
    const sqrtLambda = Math.sqrt(lambda);
    rx *= sqrtLambda;
    ry *= sqrtLambda;
  }

  // Step 2: Compute (cx', cy')
  const sign = largeArcFlag === sweepFlag ? -1 : 1;
  const sq =
    Math.max(
      0,
      rx * rx * (ry * ry) - rx * rx * (y1p * y1p) - ry * ry * (x1p * x1p)
    ) / (rx * rx * (y1p * y1p) + ry * ry * (x1p * x1p));
  const coef = sign * Math.sqrt(sq);
  const cxp = coef * ((rx * y1p) / ry);
  const cyp = coef * (-(ry * x1p) / rx);

  // Step 3: Compute (cx, cy) from (cx', cy')
  const cx = cosPhi * cxp - sinPhi * cyp + (x1 + x2) / 2;
  const cy = sinPhi * cxp + cosPhi * cyp + (y1 + y2) / 2;

  // Step 4: Compute theta1 and deltaTheta
  const v1x = (x1p - cxp) / rx;
  const v1y = (y1p - cyp) / ry;
  const v2x = (-x1p - cxp) / rx;
  const v2y = (-y1p - cyp) / ry;

  function vectorAngle(ux: number, uy: number, vx: number, vy: number): number {
    const dot = ux * vx + uy * vy;
    const len = Math.sqrt(ux * ux + uy * uy) * Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy);
    let ang = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, dot / (len || 1))));
    if (ux * vy - uy * vx < 0) ang = -ang;
    return ang;
  }

  const theta1 = vectorAngle(1, 0, v1x, v1y);
  let deltaTheta = vectorAngle(v1x, v1y, v2x, v2y);

  if (!sweepFlag && deltaTheta > 0) {
    deltaTheta -= 2 * Math.PI;
  } else if (sweepFlag && deltaTheta < 0) {
    deltaTheta += 2 * Math.PI;
  }

  // Split arc into segments of at most pi/2
  const segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.abs(deltaTheta) / (Math.PI / 2)));
  const dTheta = deltaTheta / segments;
  const halfDTheta = dTheta / 2;
  const alpha =
    (Math.sin(dTheta) * (Math.sqrt(4 + 3 * Math.tan(halfDTheta) ** 2) - 1)) / 3;

  let currentTheta = theta1;

  for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
    const nextTheta = currentTheta + dTheta;

    const cosTheta = Math.cos(currentTheta);
    const sinTheta = Math.sin(currentTheta);
    const cosNextTheta = Math.cos(nextTheta);
    const sinNextTheta = Math.sin(nextTheta);

    const ep1x = cosPhi * rx * cosTheta - sinPhi * ry * sinTheta + cx;
    const ep1y = sinPhi * rx * cosTheta + cosPhi * ry * sinTheta + cy;

    const ep2x = cosPhi * rx * cosNextTheta - sinPhi * ry * sinNextTheta + cx;
    const ep2y = sinPhi * rx * cosNextTheta + cosPhi * ry * sinNextTheta + cy;

    const dTheta1x = -cosPhi * rx * sinTheta - sinPhi * ry * cosTheta;
    const dTheta1y = -sinPhi * rx * sinTheta + cosPhi * ry * cosTheta;

    const dTheta2x = -cosPhi * rx * sinNextTheta - sinPhi * ry * cosNextTheta;
    const dTheta2y = -sinPhi * rx * sinNextTheta + cosPhi * ry * cosNextTheta;

    const cp1 = { x: ep1x + alpha * dTheta1x, y: ep1y + alpha * dTheta1y };
    const cp2 = { x: ep2x - alpha * dTheta2x, y: ep2y - alpha * dTheta2y };
    const targetPt = i === segments - 1 ? { x: x2, y: y2 } : { x: ep2x, y: ep2y };

    commands.push({
      type: "C",
      points: [cp1, cp2, targetPt],
    });

    currentTheta = nextTheta;
  }

  return commands;
}

/**
 * Compute the analytical minimum and maximum coordinates of a 1D cubic Bezier curve segment.
 */
function getCubicBezierExtrema(p0: number, p1: number, p2: number, p3: number): [number, number] {
  let min = Math.min(p0, p3);
  let max = Math.max(p0, p3);

  const a = 3 * (-p0 + 3 * p1 - 3 * p2 + p3);
  const b = 6 * (p0 - 2 * p1 + p2);
  const c = 3 * (p1 - p0);

  const testT = (t: number) => {
    if (t > 0 && t < 1) {
      const omt = 1 - t;
      const val =
        omt * omt * omt * p0 +
        3 * omt * omt * t * p1 +
        3 * omt * t * t * p2 +
        t * t * t * p3;
      if (val < min) min = val;
      if (val > max) max = val;
    }
  };

  if (Math.abs(a) < 1e-12) {
    if (Math.abs(b) > 1e-12) {
      testT(-c / b);
    }
  } else {
    const disc = b * b - 4 * a * c;
    if (disc >= 0) {
      const sqrtDisc = Math.sqrt(disc);
      testT((-b - sqrtDisc) / (2 * a));
      testT((-b + sqrtDisc) / (2 * a));
    }
  }

  return [min, max];
}

export class SvgPath {
  public commands: NormalizedPathCommand[];

  constructor(commands: NormalizedPathCommand[] = []) {
    this.commands = commands;
  }

  /**
   * Parse any SVG path string into normalized absolute commands (M, L, C, Z).
   */
  public static parse(d: string): SvgPath {
    if (!d || typeof d !== "string") {
      return new SvgPath([]);
    }

    const tokens = tokenizeSvgPath(d);
    const normalized: NormalizedPathCommand[] = [];

    let curX = 0;
    let curY = 0;
    let startX = 0;
    let startY = 0;
    let lastCpX = 0;
    let lastCpY = 0;
    let lastCmd = "";

    let idx = 0;
    while (idx < tokens.length) {
      const tok = tokens[idx];
      if (typeof tok !== "string") {
        idx++;
        continue;
      }

      let cmd = tok;
      idx++;

      while (idx < tokens.length && typeof tokens[idx] === "number") {
        const isRelative = cmd >= "a" && cmd <= "z";
        const upper = cmd.toUpperCase();

        switch (upper) {
          case "M": {
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            startX = x;
            startY = y;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = y;
            normalized.push({ type: "M", points: [{ x, y }] });
            // Subsequent coordinate pairs treated as implicit L / l
            cmd = isRelative ? "l" : "L";
            break;
          }
          case "L": {
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = y;
            normalized.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x, y }] });
            break;
          }
          case "H": {
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            curX = x;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = curY;
            normalized.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x, y: curY }] });
            break;
          }
          case "V": {
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            curY = y;
            lastCpX = curX;
            lastCpY = y;
            normalized.push({ type: "L", points: [{ x: curX, y }] });
            break;
          }
          case "C": {
            const x1 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y1 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = x2;
            lastCpY = y2;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "S": {
            // Smooth cubic: cp1 is reflection of lastCp about cur
            let x1 = curX;
            let y1 = curY;
            if (lastCmd === "C" || lastCmd === "S") {
              x1 = 2 * curX - lastCpX;
              y1 = 2 * curY - lastCpY;
            }
            const x2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y2 = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = x2;
            lastCpY = y2;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "Q": {
            // Quadratic Bezier -> convert to cubic
            const qx = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const qy = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            const x1 = curX + (2 / 3) * (qx - curX);
            const y1 = curY + (2 / 3) * (qy - curY);
            const x2 = x + (2 / 3) * (qx - x);
            const y2 = y + (2 / 3) * (qy - y);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = qx;
            lastCpY = qy;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "T": {
            // Smooth quadratic Bezier -> convert to cubic
            let qx = curX;
            let qy = curY;
            if (lastCmd === "Q" || lastCmd === "T") {
              qx = 2 * curX - lastCpX;
              qy = 2 * curY - lastCpY;
            }
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            const x1 = curX + (2 / 3) * (qx - curX);
            const y1 = curY + (2 / 3) * (qy - curY);
            const x2 = x + (2 / 3) * (qx - x);
            const y2 = y + (2 / 3) * (qy - y);

            normalized.push({
              type: "C",
              points: [
                { x: x1, y: y1 },
                { x: x2, y: y2 },
                { x, y },
              ],
            });
            lastCpX = qx;
            lastCpY = qy;
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "A": {
            const rx = tokens[idx++] as number;
            const ry = tokens[idx++] as number;
            const rot = tokens[idx++] as number;
            const largeArc = (tokens[idx++] as number) !== 0;
            const sweep = (tokens[idx++] as number) !== 0;
            const x = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curX : 0);
            const y = (tokens[idx++] as number) + (isRelative ? curY : 0);

            const arcBeziers = arcToCubicBeziers(
              curX,
              curY,
              rx,
              ry,
              rot,
              largeArc,
              sweep,
              x,
              y
            );
            for (const b of arcBeziers) {
              normalized.push(b);
            }
            curX = x;
            curY = y;
            lastCpX = x;
            lastCpY = y;
            break;
          }
          case "Z": {
            normalized.push({ type: "Z", points: [] });
            curX = startX;
            curY = startY;
            lastCpX = curX;
            lastCpY = curY;
            break;
          }
          default: {
            idx++;
            break;
          }
        }
        lastCmd = upper;
      }

      if (cmd.toUpperCase() === "Z") {
        normalized.push({ type: "Z", points: [] });
        curX = startX;
        curY = startY;
        lastCpX = curX;
        lastCpY = curY;
        lastCmd = "Z";
      }
    }

    return new SvgPath(normalized);
  }

  /**
   * Compute exact bounding box across all path commands, accounting for cubic Bezier extrema.
   */
  public getBounds(): PathBounds {
    if (this.commands.length === 0) {
      return { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
    }

    let minX = Infinity;
    let minY = Infinity;
    let maxX = -Infinity;
    let maxY = -Infinity;

    let curX = 0;
    let curY = 0;

    for (const cmd of this.commands) {
      switch (cmd.type) {
        case "M":
        case "L": {
          const pt = cmd.points[0];
          if (pt.x < minX) minX = pt.x;
          if (pt.x > maxX) maxX = pt.x;
          if (pt.y < minY) minY = pt.y;
          if (pt.y > maxY) maxY = pt.y;
          curX = pt.x;
          curY = pt.y;
          break;
        }
        case "C": {
          const [cp1, cp2, ep] = cmd.points;
          const [xMin, xMax] = getCubicBezierExtrema(curX, cp1.x, cp2.x, ep.x);
          const [yMin, yMax] = getCubicBezierExtrema(curY, cp1.y, cp2.y, ep.y);
          if (xMin < minX) minX = xMin;
          if (xMax > maxX) maxX = xMax;
          if (yMin < minY) minY = yMin;
          if (yMax > maxY) maxY = yMax;
          curX = ep.x;
          curY = ep.y;
          break;
        }
        case "Z": {
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (minX === Infinity) {
      return { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
    }

    return {
      minX,
      minY,
      maxX,
      maxY,
      width: Math.max(1e-6, maxX - minX),
      height: Math.max(1e-6, maxY - minY),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Convert normalized SVG path into a standard VML path string scaled to coordsize.
   * e.g. "m 0,0 l 1000,0 l 1000,1000 x e"
   */
  public toVmlPath(
    targetCoordWidth = 1000,
    targetCoordHeight = 1000,
    customBounds?: PathBounds
  ): string {
    if (this.commands.length === 0) {
      return "";
    }

    const bounds = customBounds || this.getBounds();
    const scaleX = targetCoordWidth / bounds.width;
    const scaleY = targetCoordHeight / bounds.height;

    const parts: string[] = [];

    for (const cmd of this.commands) {
      switch (cmd.type) {
        case "M": {
          const pt = cmd.points[0];
          const vx = Math.round((pt.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const vy = Math.round((pt.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          parts.push(`m ${vx},${vy}`);
          break;
        }
        case "L": {
          const pt = cmd.points[0];
          const vx = Math.round((pt.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const vy = Math.round((pt.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          parts.push(`l ${vx},${vy}`);
          break;
        }
        case "C": {
          const [cp1, cp2, ep] = cmd.points;
          const cx1 = Math.round((cp1.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const cy1 = Math.round((cp1.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          const cx2 = Math.round((cp2.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const cy2 = Math.round((cp2.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          const ex = Math.round((ep.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX);
          const ey = Math.round((ep.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY);
          parts.push(`c ${cx1},${cy1},${cx2},${cy2},${ex},${ey}`);
          break;
        }
        case "Z": {
          parts.push("x");
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!parts[parts.length - 1]?.endsWith("e")) {
      parts.push("e");
    }

    return parts.join(" ");
  }

  /**
   * Decomposes SVG path into discrete subpaths for HWPX, tracking whether each subpath is closed (Z).
   */
  public toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(
    targetWidthHwpu: number,
    targetHeightHwpu: number,
    stepsPerCurve = 6,
    customBounds?: PathBounds
  ): { points: Point2D[]; closed: boolean }[] {
    if (this.commands.length === 0) {
      return [];
    }

    const bounds = customBounds || this.getBounds();
    const scaleX = bounds.width > 0 ? targetWidthHwpu / bounds.width : 1;
    const scaleY = bounds.height > 0 ? targetHeightHwpu / bounds.height : 1;

    const subpaths: { points: Point2D[]; closed: boolean }[] = [];
    let currentPath: Point2D[] = [];
    let isClosed = false;
    let curX = bounds.minX;
    let curY = bounds.minY;

    for (const cmd of this.commands) {
      switch (cmd.type) {
        case "M": {
          if (currentPath.length > 0) {
            subpaths.push({ points: currentPath, closed: isClosed });
            currentPath = [];
            isClosed = false;
          }
          const p = cmd.points[0];
          curX = p.x;
          curY = p.y;
          currentPath.push({
            x: Math.round((p.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX),
            y: Math.round((p.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY),
          });
          break;
        }
        case "L": {
          const p = cmd.points[0];
          curX = p.x;
          curY = p.y;
          currentPath.push({
            x: Math.round((p.x - bounds.minX) * scaleX),
            y: Math.round((p.y - bounds.minY) * scaleY),
          });
          break;
        }
        case "C": {
          const [cp1, cp2, ep] = cmd.points;
          for (let step = 1; step <= stepsPerCurve; step++) {
            const t = step / stepsPerCurve;
            const omt = 1 - t;
            const b0 = omt * omt * omt;
            const b1 = 3 * omt * omt * t;
            const b2 = 3 * omt * t * t;
            const b3 = t * t * t;

            const px = b0 * curX + b1 * cp1.x + b2 * cp2.x + b3 * ep.x;
            const py = b0 * curY + b1 * cp1.y + b2 * cp2.y + b3 * ep.y;

            currentPath.push({
              x: Math.round((px - bounds.minX) * scaleX),
              y: Math.round((py - bounds.minY) * scaleY),
            });
          }
          curX = ep.x;
          curY = ep.y;
          break;
        }
        case "Z": {
          isClosed = true;
          if (currentPath.length > 0) {
            subpaths.push({ points: currentPath, closed: true });
            currentPath = [];
            isClosed = false;
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (currentPath.length > 0) {
      subpaths.push({ points: currentPath, closed: isClosed });
    }

    return subpaths;
  }

  /**
   * Decomposes SVG path into discrete subpaths for HWPX to avoid spurious connecting lines.
   */
  public toHwpxSubpaths(
    targetWidthHwpu: number,
    targetHeightHwpu: number,
    stepsPerCurve = 6
  ): Point2D[][] {
    return this.toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(targetWidthHwpu, targetHeightHwpu, stepsPerCurve).map(
      (sp) => sp.points
    );
  }

  /**
   * Sample path into discrete polygon vertices for HWPX (<hp:pt x="..." y="..."/>).
   * Cubic curves are sampled into intermediate vertices based on steps.
   */
  public toHwpxPolygonPoints(
    targetWidthHwpu: number,
    targetHeightHwpu: number,
    stepsPerCurve = 6
  ): Point2D[] {
    const subpaths = this.toHwpxSubpaths(targetWidthHwpu, targetHeightHwpu, stepsPerCurve);
    return subpaths.flat();
  }

  /**
   * Parse a VML path string into an SvgPath, correctly translating VML coordinate semantics.
   */
  public static fromVml(vmlPath: string): SvgPath {
    return SvgPath.parse(vmlToSvgPath(vmlPath));
  }
}

/**
 * Converts a VML path string to a standard SVG path string.
 * In VML, lowercase commands (m, l, c) are ABSOLUTE coordinates,
 * whereas in SVG lowercase commands are RELATIVE.
 * In VML, relative commands are r (rel lineto), t (rel moveto), v (rel curveto).
 * Also VML uses x for closepath and e for end of path.
 */
export function vmlToSvgPath(vmlPath: string): string {
  if (!vmlPath) return "";

  // Strip standalone formatting flags (nf = nofill, ns = nostroke)
  let s = vmlPath.replace(/\b(nf|ns)\b/gi, " ");

  // Strip path end marker 'e' (standalone or at end of segment)
  s = s.replace(/(^|[\s,])e(?=[\s,]|$)/gi, " ");

  // In VML:
  // m -> M (absolute moveto)
  // l -> L (absolute lineto)
  // c -> C (absolute curveto)
  // r -> l (relative lineto)
  // t -> m (relative moveto)
  // v -> c (relative curveto)
  // x -> Z (closepath)
  // z -> Z (closepath)
  return s.replace(/([a-df-wyzA-DF-WYZ])/g, (cmd) => {
    switch (cmd.toLowerCase()) {
      case "m": return "M";
      case "l": return "L";
      case "c": return "C";
      case "r": return "l";
      case "t": return "m";
      case "v": return "c";
      case "x":
      case "z": return "Z";
      default: return cmd;
    }
  });
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.ts
================================================================================

import {
  CanvasDocument,
  CanvasPage,
  CanvasElement,
  CanvasTextRun,
  CanvasStyle,
  ScalePolicy,
  resolveScale,
  CanvasResource,
} from "../types.js";
import { FigmaNode, FigmaFileResponse, FigmaColor, FigmaPaint } from "../../parser/types.js";
import { isA4Ratio } from "../../pipeline/index.js";
import { SvgPath } from "../geometry/svgPathParser.js";
import { decodeBase64, decodeDataUrl } from "../utils/binaryUtils.js";

export interface FigmaCanvasParserOptions {
  imageMap?: Record<string, string>;
  selectedFrameIds?: string[];
  scalePolicy?: ScalePolicy;
  titleOverride?: string;
}

export class FigmaCanvasParser {
  private imageMap: Record<string, string>;
  private scalePolicy: ScalePolicy;
  private selectedFrameIds?: Set<string>;
  private titleOverride?: string;
  private resources: Record<string, CanvasResource> = {};
  private imageCounter = 0;
  private imageRefMap = new Map<string, string>(); // figmaRef -> resourceId

  constructor(options?: FigmaCanvasParserOptions) {
    this.imageMap = options?.imageMap || {};
    this.scalePolicy = options?.scalePolicy || "fit-a4";
    this.selectedFrameIds = options?.selectedFrameIds
      ? new Set(options.selectedFrameIds)
      : undefined;
    this.titleOverride = options?.titleOverride;
  }

  public parse(input: FigmaNode | FigmaFileResponse | any): CanvasDocument {
    this.resources = {};
    this.imageCounter = 0;
    this.imageRefMap.clear();

    if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) {
      throw new Error("Invalid Figma JSON input: expected a JSON object representing a Figma node, file response, or snapshot.");
    }

    if ("imageMap" in input && input.imageMap && typeof input.imageMap === "object") {
      this.imageMap = { ...input.imageMap, ...this.imageMap };
    }

    let rootNode: FigmaNode;
    if ("figmaNode" in input && input.figmaNode && typeof input.figmaNode === "object") {
      rootNode = input.figmaNode;
    } else if ("document" in input && input.document && typeof input.document === "object") {
      rootNode = input.document;
    } else if ("nodes" in input && input.nodes && typeof input.nodes === "object") {
      const firstEntry = Object.values(input.nodes)[0] as any;
      rootNode = firstEntry?.document || firstEntry;
    } else {
      rootNode = input as FigmaNode;
    }

    if (!rootNode || typeof rootNode !== "object" || (!rootNode.type && !Array.isArray(rootNode.children))) {
      throw new Error("Invalid Figma JSON structure: missing valid root Figma node ('type' or 'children').");
    }

    const documentName =
      this.titleOverride ||
      (input.metadata?.fileName ? String(input.metadata.fileName) : "") ||
      ("name" in input && input.name ? String(input.name) : "") ||
      (rootNode.name ? String(rootNode.name) : "Figma Document");

    const notices: string[] = [];
    const warnings: string[] = [];

    // Step 1: Discover top-level frames representing document pages
    const pageFrames = this.discoverPageFrames(rootNode);

    if (pageFrames.length === 0) {
      warnings.push("No candidate page frames found. Using root as single page.");
    }

    const pages: CanvasPage[] = [];

    // If no page frames found, treat root itself as a page
    const framesToProcess = pageFrames.length > 0 ? pageFrames : [rootNode];

    for (let i = 0; i < framesToProcess.length; i++) {
      const frame = framesToProcess[i];
      const pageIndex = i + 1;
      const page = this.parsePageFrame(frame, pageIndex, notices, warnings);
      pages.push(page);
    }

    const primaryWidthPt = pages.length > 0 ? pages[0].bounds.width : 595.28;
    const primaryHeightPt = pages.length > 0 ? pages[0].bounds.height : 841.89;
    const isLandscape = primaryWidthPt > primaryHeightPt;
    const isA4 = Math.abs(primaryWidthPt * primaryHeightPt - 595.28 * 841.89) < 200;
    const pageSizeName = isA4 ? "A4" : "Custom";

    return {
      title: documentName,
      pageSize: {
        name: pageSizeName,
        widthPt: Number(primaryWidthPt.toFixed(2)),
        heightPt: Number(primaryHeightPt.toFixed(2)),
        orientation: isLandscape ? "landscape" : "portrait",
        unit: "pt",
      },
      marginsPt: {
        top: 0,
        right: 0,
        bottom: 0,
        left: 0,
      },
      pages,
      resources: this.resources,
      diagnostics: {
        notices,
        warnings,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Discovers top-level frames that qualify as document pages (e.g. A4 frames).
   */
  private discoverPageFrames(root: FigmaNode): FigmaNode[] {
    const candidateFrames: FigmaNode[] = [];

    const visitCanvasOrDoc = (node: FigmaNode) => {
      if (!node.children) return;

      if (node.type === "CANVAS") {
        const visChildren = node.children.filter((c) => c.visible !== false);
        const frameChildren = visChildren.filter(
          (c) => c.type === "FRAME" || c.type === "COMPONENT" || c.type === "GROUP"
        );
        const leafChildren = visChildren.filter(
          (c) =>
            c.type === "TEXT" ||
            c.type === "LINE" ||
            c.type === "RECTANGLE" ||
            c.type === "VECTOR" ||
            c.type === "ELLIPSE"
        );
        const allFramesSelected =
          !this.selectedFrameIds ||
          frameChildren.every((f) => this.selectedFrameIds!.has(f.id));
        const allFramesAreShortSections =
          frameChildren.length >= 1 &&
          frameChildren.every(
            (f) =>
              (f.absoluteBoundingBox?.height || 0) > 0 &&
              (f.absoluteBoundingBox?.height || 0) < 500
          );
        const hasMixedLeafAndFrames = leafChildren.length > 0 && frameChildren.length > 0;

        if (
          allFramesSelected &&
          allFramesAreShortSections &&
          (hasMixedLeafAndFrames || frameChildren.length >= 2)
        ) {
          let minX = Infinity;
          let minY = Infinity;
          let maxX = -Infinity;
          let maxY = -Infinity;
          for (const c of visChildren) {
            const b = c.absoluteBoundingBox;
            if (b) {
              if (b.x < minX) minX = b.x;
              if (b.y < minY) minY = b.y;
              if (b.x + b.width > maxX) maxX = b.x + b.width;
              if (b.y + b.height > maxY) maxY = b.y + b.height;
            }
          }
          if (!isFinite(minX)) {
            minX = 0;
            minY = 0;
            maxX = 794;
            maxY = 1123;
          }
          const spanW = Math.max(794, maxX - minX);
          const spanH = Math.max(maxY - minY + 48, Math.round(spanW * (841.89 / 595.28)));
          candidateFrames.push({
            ...node,
            type: "FRAME",
            absoluteBoundingBox: { x: minX, y: minY, width: spanW, height: spanH },
            children: visChildren,
          });
          return;
        }
      }

      for (const child of node.children) {
        if (child.visible === false) continue;

        if (child.type === "CANVAS") {
          visitCanvasOrDoc(child);
          continue;
        }

        // If frame selection filter is active
        if (this.selectedFrameIds && !this.selectedFrameIds.has(child.id)) {
          continue;
        }

        // Check if child is a frame or component
        if (
          child.type === "FRAME" ||
          child.type === "COMPONENT" ||
          child.type === "GROUP"
        ) {
          candidateFrames.push(child);
        }
      }
    };

    if (root.type === "DOCUMENT" || root.type === "CANVAS") {
      visitCanvasOrDoc(root);
    } else {
      candidateFrames.push(root);
    }

    // Sort candidate frames in reading order (top-to-bottom, left-to-right)
    candidateFrames.sort((a, b) => {
      const boxA = a.absoluteBoundingBox || { x: 0, y: 0 };
      const boxB = b.absoluteBoundingBox || { x: 0, y: 0 };
      const rowA = Math.round(boxA.y / 100);
      const rowB = Math.round(boxB.y / 100);
      if (rowA !== rowB) return rowA - rowB;
      return boxA.x - boxB.x;
    });

    return candidateFrames;
  }

  /**
   * Synthesizes deterministic absoluteBoundingBox coordinates for child nodes inside
   * Auto-Layout frames (layoutMode: VERTICAL | HORIZONTAL) when absoluteBoundingBox is omitted.
   */
  private synthesizeMissingChildBounds(
    parent: FigmaNode,
    parentBox: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }
  ): void {
    if (!parent.children || parent.children.length === 0) return;

    const pl = (parent as any).paddingLeft || 0;
    const pr = (parent as any).paddingRight || 0;
    const pt = (parent as any).paddingTop || 0;
    const pb = (parent as any).paddingBottom || 0;
    const gap = (parent as any).itemSpacing || 12;
    const layoutMode = (parent as any).layoutMode || "VERTICAL";

    const innerX = parentBox.x + pl;
    const innerY = parentBox.y + pt;
    const innerW = Math.max(20, parentBox.width - pl - pr);
    const innerH = Math.max(16, parentBox.height - pt - pb);

    const visChildren = parent.children.filter((c) => c.visible !== false);
    if (visChildren.length === 0) return;

    if (layoutMode === "HORIZONTAL") {
      let cursorX = innerX;
      const missingCount = visChildren.filter((c) => !c.absoluteBoundingBox).length;
      const usedW = visChildren.reduce(
        (acc, c) => acc + (c.absoluteBoundingBox ? c.absoluteBoundingBox.width + gap : 0),
        0
      );
      const remW = Math.max(40, innerW - usedW);
      const defaultColW =
        missingCount > 0
          ? Math.max(40, (remW - gap * Math.max(0, missingCount - 1)) / missingCount)
          : innerW;

      for (const child of visChildren) {
        if (!child.absoluteBoundingBox) {
          let cw = defaultColW;
          let ch = innerH;
          if (child.type === "TEXT") {
            const fs = child.style?.fontSize || 12;
            const lh = child.style?.lineHeightPx || Math.round(fs * 1.35);
            ch = Math.min(innerH, Math.max(lh + 4, innerH));
          }
          (child as any).absoluteBoundingBox = {
            x: cursorX,
            y: innerY,
            width: cw,
            height: ch,
          };
        }
        const cb = child.absoluteBoundingBox!;
        cursorX = Math.max(cursorX, cb.x + cb.width + gap);
        this.synthesizeMissingChildBounds(child, cb);
      }
    } else {
      let cursorY = innerY;
      for (const child of visChildren) {
        if (!child.absoluteBoundingBox) {
          let cw = innerW;
          let ch = 32;

          if (child.type === "TEXT") {
            const fs = child.style?.fontSize || 12;
            const lh = child.style?.lineHeightPx || Math.round(fs * 1.4);
            const chars = child.characters || "";
            const charsPerLine = Math.max(8, Math.floor(innerW / (fs * 0.72)));
            const explicitLines = chars.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
            const totalLines = explicitLines.reduce(
              (sum, l) => sum + Math.max(1, Math.ceil(l.length / charsPerLine)),
              0
            );
            cw = innerW;
            ch = Math.max(lh + 4, totalLines * lh + 4);
          } else if (
            child.type === "FRAME" ||
            child.type === "COMPONENT" ||
            child.type === "GROUP"
          ) {
            const cpl = (child as any).paddingLeft || 0;
            const cpr = (child as any).paddingRight || 0;
            const cpt = (child as any).paddingTop || 0;
            const cpb = (child as any).paddingBottom || 0;
            if (
              (child as any).primaryAxisSizingMode === "AUTO" &&
              child.children &&
              child.children.length === 1 &&
              child.children[0].type === "TEXT"
            ) {
              const tNode = child.children[0];
              const fs = tNode.style?.fontSize || 12;
              const lh = tNode.style?.lineHeightPx || Math.round(fs * 1.35);
              const tLen = (tNode.characters || "").length;
              cw = Math.min(innerW, Math.max(60, Math.round(tLen * fs * 0.8 + cpl + cpr + 12)));
              ch = lh + cpt + cpb + 4;
            } else {
              cw = innerW;
              ch = Math.max(48, Math.min(200, innerH - Math.max(0, cursorY - innerY)));
            }
          }

          (child as any).absoluteBoundingBox = {
            x: innerX,
            y: cursorY,
            width: cw,
            height: ch,
          };
        }
        const cb = child.absoluteBoundingBox!;
        cursorY = Math.max(cursorY, cb.y + cb.height + gap);
        this.synthesizeMissingChildBounds(child, cb);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Parses a single top-level frame into a CanvasPage.
   */
  private parsePageFrame(
    frame: FigmaNode,
    pageNumber: number,
    notices: string[],
    warnings: string[]
  ): CanvasPage {
    const frameBox = frame.absoluteBoundingBox || {
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      width: 794,
      height: 1123,
    };

    // Synthesize bounds for Auto-Layout child nodes that omit explicit absoluteBoundingBox
    this.synthesizeMissingChildBounds(frame, frameBox);

    const resolved = resolveScale(
      frameBox.width,
      frameBox.height,
      this.scalePolicy
    );
    const { scaleX, scaleY, targetWidthPt, targetHeightPt } = resolved;

    // Check background color
    let backgroundColor: string | undefined;
    const rawBg = (frame as any).backgroundColor;
    if (rawBg) {
      backgroundColor = this.figmaColorToHex(rawBg);
    } else if (frame.fills && frame.fills.length > 0) {
      const solid = frame.fills.find(
        (f) => f.type === "SOLID" && f.visible !== false
      );
      if (solid && solid.color) {
        backgroundColor = this.figmaColorToHex(solid.color);
      }
    }

    const elements: CanvasElement[] = [];
    let currentZIndex = 1;

    // Helper to recursively process children
    const processChildren = (node: FigmaNode) => {
      if (!node.children) return;

      for (const child of node.children) {
        if (child.visible === false) continue;

        const childBox = child.absoluteBoundingBox;
        if (!childBox) {
          // If no absolute bounding box, still process children if any
          processChildren(child);
          continue;
        }

        // Relative coordinate to page frame
        const relX = childBox.x - frameBox.x;
        const relY = childBox.y - frameBox.y;

        const xPt = relX * scaleX;
        const yPt = relY * scaleY;
        const wPt = childBox.width * scaleX;
        const hPt = childBox.height * scaleY;

        // Diagnostic: clipsContent check
        if (child.clipsContent) {
          notices.push(
            `Page ${pageNumber}, node ${child.name || child.id} has clipsContent=true.`
          );
        }

        // Check if node is an image fill
        const imageFill = child.fills?.find(
          (f) => f.type === "IMAGE" && f.visible !== false && f.imageRef
        );

        if (imageFill && imageFill.imageRef) {
          const resId = this.resolveImageResource(imageFill.imageRef);
          const scaleModeRaw = (imageFill as any).scaleMode as
            | "FILL"
            | "FIT"
            | "CROP"
            | "TILE"
            | undefined;
          const imageScaleMode = scaleModeRaw || "FILL";
          let imageCrop:
            | { top: number; bottom: number; left: number; right: number }
            | undefined;

          const transform = (imageFill as any).imageTransform as
            | [[number, number, number], [number, number, number]]
            | undefined;
          if (
            imageScaleMode === "CROP" &&
            Array.isArray(transform) &&
            transform.length === 2 &&
            Array.isArray(transform[0]) &&
            Array.isArray(transform[1])
          ) {
            const sx = transform[0][0] ?? 1;
            const tx = transform[0][2] ?? 0;
            const sy = transform[1][1] ?? 1;
            const ty = transform[1][2] ?? 0;
            let left = 0,
              top = 0,
              right = 0,
              bottom = 0;
            if (tx >= 0 && ty >= 0 && sx > 0 && sx <= 1 && sy > 0 && sy <= 1) {
              left = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, tx));
              top = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, ty));
              right = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (tx + sx)));
              bottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (ty + sy)));
            } else if (sx > 0 && sy > 0) {
              left = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, -tx / sx));
              top = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, -ty / sy));
              right = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (1 - tx) / sx));
              bottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, 1 - (1 - ty) / sy));
            }
            if (left > 0 || top > 0 || right > 0 || bottom > 0) {
              imageCrop = { top, bottom, left, right };
            }
          }

          elements.push({
            id: child.id,
            name: child.name,
            type: "image",
            bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
            zIndex: currentZIndex++,
            rotation: (child as any).rotation,
            opacity: child.opacity,
            style: this.extractStyle(child, scaleX),
            imageRef: resId,
            imageScaleMode,
            imageCrop,
            sourceNodeId: child.id,
            sourceNodeType: child.type,
          });
        } else if (child.type === "TEXT") {
          const textRuns = this.extractTextRuns(child, scaleX);
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const align = this.extractTextAlign(child);

          let verticalAlign: "top" | "center" | "bottom" | undefined;
          const vAlignRaw = child.style?.textAlignVertical || (child as any).textAlignVertical;
          if (vAlignRaw === "CENTER") {
            verticalAlign = "center";
          } else if (vAlignRaw === "BOTTOM") {
            verticalAlign = "bottom";
          } else if (vAlignRaw === "TOP") {
            verticalAlign = "top";
          }

          let isCenteredInParent = false;
          let textYPt = yPt;
          let textHPt = hPt;
          const parentBox = node.absoluteBoundingBox;

          // Check if text is vertically centered in parent container (badge, button, table cell, row)
          if (
            node.id !== frame.id &&
            parentBox &&
            childBox &&
            parentBox.height < 600 &&
            parentBox.height > childBox.height + 2
          ) {
            const topGap = childBox.y - parentBox.y;
            const bottomGap = (parentBox.y + parentBox.height) - (childBox.y + childBox.height);
            const isSymmetric = Math.abs(topGap - bottomGap) <= 2.5;

            // Only consider centered if parent is NOT a vertical stack of multiple children
            const isVerticalStack =
              (node as any).layoutMode === "VERTICAL" &&
              node.children &&
              node.children.length > 1;

            if (isSymmetric && !isVerticalStack) {
              isCenteredInParent = true;
              verticalAlign = "center";
              const parentRelY = parentBox.y - frameBox.y;
              textYPt = parentRelY * scaleY;
              textHPt = parentBox.height * scaleY;
            }
          }

          // For Figma TEXT nodes, 'fills' represents character font colors (stored in textRuns),
          // NOT a container background fill. Remove fillColor so the text box has a transparent background.
          delete style.fillColor;
          delete style.fillOpacity;

          const padTop = ((child as any).paddingTop || 0) * scaleY;
          const padRight = ((child as any).paddingRight || 0) * scaleX;
          const padBottom = ((child as any).paddingBottom || 0) * scaleY;
          const padLeft = ((child as any).paddingLeft || 0) * scaleX;
          const innerPaddingPt =
            padTop > 0 || padRight > 0 || padBottom > 0 || padLeft > 0
              ? { top: padTop, right: padRight, bottom: padBottom, left: padLeft }
              : undefined;

          const rawTar = (child.style as any)?.textAutoResize || (child as any).textAutoResize;
          const textAutoResize: "NONE" | "HEIGHT" | "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT" | "TRUNCATE" | undefined =
            rawTar === "HEIGHT" || rawTar === "WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT" || rawTar === "NONE" || rawTar === "TRUNCATE"
              ? rawTar
              : undefined;

          elements.push({
            id: child.id,
            name: child.name,
            type: "text",
            bounds: { x: xPt, y: textYPt, width: wPt, height: textHPt },
            zIndex: currentZIndex++,
            rotation: (child as any).rotation,
            opacity: child.opacity,
            style,
            textRuns,
            paragraphAlign: align,
            verticalAlign,
            innerPaddingPt,
            textAutoResize,
            contentHeightPt: isCenteredInParent ? hPt : undefined,
            sourceNodeId: child.id,
            sourceNodeType: child.type,
          });
        } else if (child.type === "LINE") {
          let lineW = wPt;
          let lineH = hPt;
          const rawRot = (child as any).rotation;
          if (typeof rawRot === "number" && lineW > 0 && lineH === 0) {
            const absRot = Math.abs(rawRot);
            if (Math.abs(absRot - Math.PI / 2) < 0.1 || Math.abs(absRot - 90) < 5) {
              lineH = lineW;
              lineW = 0;
            }
          }
          elements.push({
            id: child.id,
            name: child.name,
            type: "line",
            bounds: {
              x: xPt,
              y: yPt,
              width: lineW,
              height: lineW === 0 && lineH > 0 ? lineH : Math.max(lineH, 1),
            },
            zIndex: currentZIndex++,
            rotation: rawRot,
            opacity: child.opacity,
            style: this.extractStyle(child, scaleX),
            sourceNodeId: child.id,
            sourceNodeType: child.type,
          });
        } else if (child.type === "ELLIPSE") {
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const hasFill = !!style.fillColor;
          const hasStroke = !!style.strokeColor && (style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;
          if (hasFill || hasStroke) {
            const arcData = (child as any).arcData as
              | { startingAngle?: number; endingAngle?: number; innerRadius?: number }
              | undefined;
            const innerRadius = arcData?.innerRadius ?? 0;
            const startAngle = arcData?.startingAngle ?? 0;
            const endAngle = arcData?.endingAngle ?? Math.PI * 2;
            const sweepDelta = endAngle - startAngle;
            const isPartialArc =
              arcData !== undefined &&
              Math.abs(Math.abs(sweepDelta) - Math.PI * 2) > 0.01;
            const isDonutOrSector = innerRadius > 0.001 || isPartialArc;

            if (isDonutOrSector && Math.abs(sweepDelta) > 1e-4) {
              const rawPath = this.buildEllipseArcSvgPath(
                startAngle,
                endAngle,
                innerRadius
              );
              const parsed = SvgPath.parse(rawPath);
              const unitBounds = {
                minX: 0,
                minY: 0,
                maxX: 1000,
                maxY: 1000,
                width: 1000,
                height: 1000,
              };
              const vmlPath = parsed.toVmlPath(1000, 1000, unitBounds);
              const wHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(wPt * 100));
              const hHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(hPt * 100));
              const vectorSubpaths = parsed.toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(
                wHwpu,
                hHwpu,
                6,
                unitBounds
              );
              const vectorPoints = vectorSubpaths.flatMap((s) => s.points);

              elements.push({
                id: child.id,
                name: child.name,
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: (child as any).rotation,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style,
                vectorPath: vmlPath,
                rawSvgPath: rawPath,
                vectorPoints,
                vectorSubpaths,
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            } else {
              elements.push({
                id: child.id,
                name: child.name,
                type: "oval",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: (child as any).rotation,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style,
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
          }
          processChildren(child);
        } else if (
          child.type === "VECTOR" ||
          child.type === "STAR" ||
          child.type === "POLYGON" ||
          child.type === "BOOLEAN_OPERATION"
        ) {
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const hasFill = !!style.fillColor;
          const hasStroke = !!style.strokeColor && (style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

          if (hasFill || hasStroke) {
            const parentName = node.name || "";
            const nodeName = child.name || "";
            let rawPath = "";

            if ((child as any).fillGeometry?.[0]?.path) {
              rawPath = (child as any).fillGeometry[0].path;
            } else if ((child as any).strokeGeometry?.[0]?.path) {
              rawPath = (child as any).strokeGeometry[0].path;
            } else if ((child as any).vectorPaths?.[0]?.data) {
              rawPath = (child as any).vectorPaths[0].data;
            } else if (child.type === "STAR") {
              rawPath = "M 500 0 L 618 353 L 990 353 L 689 572 L 804 925 L 500 706 L 196 925 L 311 572 L 10 353 L 382 353 Z";
            } else if (child.type === "POLYGON") {
              rawPath = "M 500 0 L 1000 866 L 0 866 Z";
            } else {
              // Standard UI icon path normalization (SVG format)
              const nameLower = (parentName + " " + nodeName).toLowerCase();
              if (nameLower.includes("alert") || nameLower.includes("warn") || nameLower.includes("triangle")) {
                rawPath = "M 500 60 L 950 920 L 50 920 Z M 500 380 L 500 640 M 500 780 L 500 840";
              } else if (nameLower.includes("check") || nameLower.includes("tick")) {
                rawPath = "M 150 520 L 400 780 L 880 180";
              } else if (nameLower.includes("info")) {
                rawPath = "M 500 50 C 748 50 950 252 950 500 C 950 748 748 950 500 950 C 252 950 50 748 50 500 C 50 252 252 50 500 50 Z M 500 280 L 500 340 M 500 460 L 500 740";
              } else if (nameLower.includes("file") || nameLower.includes("doc")) {
                rawPath = "M 200 80 L 620 80 L 820 280 L 820 920 L 200 920 Z M 620 80 L 620 280 L 820 280 M 340 480 L 680 480 M 340 640 L 680 640";
              }
            }

            let vmlPath = "";
            let vectorPoints: { x: number; y: number }[] | undefined;
            let vectorSubpaths: { points: { x: number; y: number }[]; closed: boolean }[] | undefined;

            if (rawPath) {
              const parsed = SvgPath.parse(rawPath);
              vmlPath = parsed.toVmlPath(1000, 1000);
              const wHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(wPt * 100));
              const hHwpu = Math.max(10, Math.round(hPt * 100));
              vectorSubpaths = parsed.toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(wHwpu, hHwpu);
              vectorPoints = vectorSubpaths.flatMap((s) => s.points);
            }

            elements.push({
              id: child.id,
              name: child.name,
              type: "vector",
              bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
              zIndex: currentZIndex++,
              rotation: (child as any).rotation,
              opacity: child.opacity,
              style,
              vectorPath: vmlPath,
              rawSvgPath: rawPath,
              vectorPoints,
              vectorSubpaths,
              sourceNodeId: child.id,
              sourceNodeType: child.type,
            });
          }
          processChildren(child);
        } else if (
          child.type === "RECTANGLE" ||
          child.type === "FRAME" ||
          child.type === "COMPONENT" ||
          child.type === "INSTANCE"
        ) {
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          const hasFill = !!style.fillColor;
          const hasStroke = !!style.strokeColor && (style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

          const bs = style.borderSides;
          const isPartialBorder =
            hasStroke &&
            bs !== undefined &&
            ((bs.top ?? 0) === 0 ||
              (bs.right ?? 0) === 0 ||
              (bs.bottom ?? 0) === 0 ||
              (bs.left ?? 0) === 0);

          if (isPartialBorder) {
            // 1. If has background fill, emit a card with fill only (no 4-sided stroke)
            if (hasFill) {
              elements.push({
                id: child.id,
                name: child.name,
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: (child as any).rotation,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  fillColor: style.fillColor,
                  fillOpacity: style.fillOpacity,
                  cornerRadius: style.cornerRadius,
                  cornerRadii: style.cornerRadii,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }

            // 2. Emit separate line elements for the active border sides
            const strokeColor = style.strokeColor;
            const strokeDashed = style.strokeDashed;

            if ((bs.bottom ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_b`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_bottom`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt + hPt, width: wPt, height: 1 },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.bottom,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
            if ((bs.top ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_t`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_top`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: 1 },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.top,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
            if ((bs.left ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_l`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_left`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: 1, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.left,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
            if ((bs.right ?? 0) > 0) {
              elements.push({
                id: `${child.id}_r`,
                name: `${child.name || "border"}_right`,
                type: "line",
                bounds: { x: xPt + wPt, y: yPt, width: 1, height: hPt },
                zIndex: currentZIndex++,
                rotation: 0,
                opacity: child.opacity,
                style: {
                  strokeColor,
                  strokeWidth: bs.right,
                  strokeDashed,
                },
                sourceNodeId: child.id,
                sourceNodeType: child.type,
              });
            }
          } else if (hasFill || hasStroke) {
            elements.push({
              id: child.id,
              name: child.name,
              type: "card",
              bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
              zIndex: currentZIndex++,
              rotation: (child as any).rotation,
              opacity: child.opacity,
              style,
              sourceNodeId: child.id,
              sourceNodeType: child.type,
            });
          }

          // Process nested children inside this container
          processChildren(child);
        } else if (child.type === "GROUP") {
          processChildren(child);
        } else {
          // Fallback for VECTOR, STAR, ELLIPSE, etc.
          const style = this.extractStyle(child, scaleX);
          if (style.fillColor || style.strokeColor) {
            elements.push({
              id: child.id,
              name: child.name,
              type: "card",
              bounds: { x: xPt, y: yPt, width: wPt, height: hPt },
              zIndex: currentZIndex++,
              rotation: (child as any).rotation,
              opacity: child.opacity,
              style,
              sourceNodeId: child.id,
              sourceNodeType: child.type,
            });
          }
          processChildren(child);
        }
      }
    };

    processChildren(frame);

    // Sort elements by zIndex
    elements.sort((a, b) => a.zIndex - b.zIndex);

    return {
      pageNumber,
      name: frame.name || `Page ${pageNumber}`,
      bounds: {
        x: 0,
        y: 0,
        width: targetWidthPt,
        height: targetHeightPt,
      },
      backgroundColor,
      elements,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Resolves a Figma imageRef to a local CanvasResource.
   */
  private resolveImageResource(imageRef: string): string {
    if (this.imageRefMap.has(imageRef)) {
      return this.imageRefMap.get(imageRef)!;
    }

    this.imageCounter++;
    const resId = `image${this.imageCounter}.png`;
    const dataUrl = this.imageMap[imageRef];

    let data: Uint8Array;
    let mimeType = "image/png";

    if (dataUrl && dataUrl.startsWith("data:")) {
      const decoded = decodeDataUrl(dataUrl);
      if (decoded) {
        mimeType = decoded.mime || "image/png";
        data = decoded.bytes;
      } else {
        data = new Uint8Array(0);
      }
    } else {
      // 1x1 transparent placeholder
      data = decodeBase64(
        "iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAQAAAC1HAwCAAAAC0lEQVR42mNkYAAAAAYAAjCB0C8AAAAASUVORK5CYII="
      );
    }

    this.resources[resId] = {
      id: resId,
      name: resId,
      mimeType,
      data,
    };

    this.imageRefMap.set(imageRef, resId);
    return resId;
  }

  /**
   * Extracts visual style (fill, stroke, corner radius) from a Figma node.
   */
  private extractStyle(node: FigmaNode, scale: number): CanvasStyle {
    const style: CanvasStyle = {};

    // 1. Fills
    if (node.fills && node.fills.length > 0) {
      const solid = node.fills.find(
        (f) => f.type === "SOLID" && f.visible !== false
      );
      if (solid && solid.color) {
        style.fillColor = this.figmaColorToHex(solid.color);
        style.fillOpacity = solid.opacity ?? solid.color.a ?? 1.0;
      }
    }

    // 2. Strokes
    const rawWeight = typeof node.strokeWeight === "number" ? node.strokeWeight : 1;
    const isw = (node as any).individualStrokeWeights as
      | { top?: number; right?: number; bottom?: number; left?: number }
      | undefined;

    let hasNonZeroWeight = rawWeight > 0;
    if (isw) {
      style.borderSides = {
        top: typeof isw.top === "number" ? isw.top * scale : undefined,
        right: typeof isw.right === "number" ? isw.right * scale : undefined,
        bottom: typeof isw.bottom === "number" ? isw.bottom * scale : undefined,
        left: typeof isw.left === "number" ? isw.left * scale : undefined,
      };
      hasNonZeroWeight =
        (style.borderSides.top ?? 0) > 0 ||
        (style.borderSides.right ?? 0) > 0 ||
        (style.borderSides.bottom ?? 0) > 0 ||
        (style.borderSides.left ?? 0) > 0;
    }

    if (hasNonZeroWeight && node.strokes && node.strokes.length > 0) {
      const stroke = node.strokes.find(
        (s) => s.type === "SOLID" && s.visible !== false
      );
      if (stroke && stroke.color) {
        style.strokeColor = this.figmaColorToHex(stroke.color);
        style.strokeWidth = rawWeight * scale;
        const dashes = (node as any).strokeDashes;
        if (dashes && dashes.length > 0) {
          style.strokeDashed = true;
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. Corner Radius
    if (
      Array.isArray(node.rectangleCornerRadii) &&
      node.rectangleCornerRadii.length === 4
    ) {
      const radii = node.rectangleCornerRadii.map((r) => (r || 0) * scale) as [
        number,
        number,
        number,
        number
      ];
      style.cornerRadii = radii;
      const maxR = Math.max(...radii);
      if (maxR > 0) {
        const avgR = (radii[0] + radii[1] + radii[2] + radii[3]) / 4;
        style.cornerRadius = avgR > 0 ? avgR : maxR;
      }
    }
    if (node.cornerRadius) {
      style.cornerRadius = node.cornerRadius * scale;
    }

    return style;
  }

  /**
   * Builds an exact SVG path in [0..1000, 0..1000] coordinate space for an ELLIPSE node
   * that defines Figma arcData (donut sector or pie slice).
   */
  private buildEllipseArcSvgPath(
    startAngle: number,
    endAngle: number,
    innerRadius: number
  ): string {
    const cx = 500;
    const cy = 500;
    const outerR = 500;
    const innerR = Math.max(0, Math.min(499, outerR * innerRadius));
    const delta = endAngle - startAngle;
    const segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.abs(delta) / (Math.PI / 2)));
    const dTheta = delta / segments;
    const alpha = (4 / 3) * Math.tan(dTheta / 4);

    const parts: string[] = [];

    const x0 = cx + outerR * Math.cos(startAngle);
    const y0 = cy + outerR * Math.sin(startAngle);
    parts.push(`M ${x0.toFixed(3)} ${y0.toFixed(3)}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
      const t0 = startAngle + i * dTheta;
      const t1 = t0 + dTheta;
      const p0x = cx + outerR * Math.cos(t0);
      const p0y = cy + outerR * Math.sin(t0);
      const p1x = cx + outerR * Math.cos(t1);
      const p1y = cy + outerR * Math.sin(t1);
      const cp1x = p0x - alpha * outerR * Math.sin(t0);
      const cp1y = p0y + alpha * outerR * Math.cos(t0);
      const cp2x = p1x + alpha * outerR * Math.sin(t1);
      const cp2y = p1y - alpha * outerR * Math.cos(t1);
      parts.push(
        `C ${cp1x.toFixed(3)} ${cp1y.toFixed(3)} ${cp2x.toFixed(3)} ${cp2y.toFixed(3)} ${p1x.toFixed(3)} ${p1y.toFixed(3)}`
      );
    }

    if (innerR > 0.5) {
      const ixEnd = cx + innerR * Math.cos(endAngle);
      const iyEnd = cy + innerR * Math.sin(endAngle);
      parts.push(`L ${ixEnd.toFixed(3)} ${iyEnd.toFixed(3)}`);

      const revAlpha = -alpha;
      for (let i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
        const t0 = endAngle - i * dTheta;
        const t1 = t0 - dTheta;
        const p0x = cx + innerR * Math.cos(t0);
        const p0y = cy + innerR * Math.sin(t0);
        const p1x = cx + innerR * Math.cos(t1);
        const p1y = cy + innerR * Math.sin(t1);
        const cp1x = p0x - revAlpha * innerR * Math.sin(t0);
        const cp1y = p0y + revAlpha * innerR * Math.cos(t0);
        const cp2x = p1x + revAlpha * innerR * Math.sin(t1);
        const cp2y = p1y - revAlpha * innerR * Math.cos(t1);
        parts.push(
          `C ${cp1x.toFixed(3)} ${cp1y.toFixed(3)} ${cp2x.toFixed(3)} ${cp2y.toFixed(3)} ${p1x.toFixed(3)} ${p1y.toFixed(3)}`
        );
      }
      parts.push("Z");
    } else {
      parts.push(`L ${cx} ${cy} Z`);
    }

    return parts.join(" ");
  }

  /**
   * Extracts text runs from a Figma TEXT node, taking into account
   * character style overrides if present.
   */
  private extractTextRuns(node: FigmaNode, scale: number): CanvasTextRun[] {
    const characters = node.characters || "";
    if (!characters) return [];

    const defaultFontFamily = node.style?.fontFamily || "Inter";
    const defaultFontSize = (node.style?.fontSize || 12) * scale;
    const rawWeight = node.style?.fontWeight;
    const defaultFontWeight =
      typeof rawWeight === "number"
        ? rawWeight
        : rawWeight
        ? parseInt(String(rawWeight), 10) || 400
        : 400;
    const defaultItalic =
      node.style?.italic ||
      node.style?.fontPostScriptName?.toLowerCase().includes("italic") ||
      false;
    const defaultUnderline = node.style?.textDecoration === "UNDERLINE";
    const defaultStrike = node.style?.textDecoration === "STRIKETHROUGH";

    const lineHeightUnit = (node.style as any)?.lineHeightUnit as string | undefined;
    let defaultLineHeightPt: number | undefined;
    if (lineHeightUnit !== "INTRINSIC_%") {
      if (typeof node.style?.lineHeightPx === "number" && node.style.lineHeightPx > 0) {
        defaultLineHeightPt = node.style.lineHeightPx * scale;
      } else if (
        typeof (node.style as any)?.lineHeightPercentFontSize === "number" &&
        (node.style as any).lineHeightPercentFontSize > 0
      ) {
        defaultLineHeightPt =
          defaultFontSize * ((node.style as any).lineHeightPercentFontSize / 100);
      }
    }

    const defaultLetterSpacingPt =
      typeof node.style?.letterSpacing === "number" && node.style.letterSpacing !== 0
        ? node.style.letterSpacing * scale
        : undefined;

    // Text color from fills (or style.fills fallback in simplified fixtures)
    let defaultColor = "#000000";
    const textFills =
      node.fills && node.fills.length > 0
        ? node.fills
        : ((node.style as any)?.fills as any[] | undefined);
    if (textFills && textFills.length > 0) {
      const solid = textFills.find(
        (f) => f.type === "SOLID" && f.visible !== false
      );
      if (solid && solid.color) {
        defaultColor = this.figmaColorToHex(solid.color);
      }
    }

    const runs: CanvasTextRun[] = [];

    // Check for characterStyleOverrides
    const overrides = node.characterStyleOverrides;
    const styleTable = node.styleOverrideTable;

    if (
      overrides &&
      Array.isArray(overrides) &&
      overrides.length > 0 &&
      styleTable
    ) {
      let currentRunText = "";
      let currentStyleKey: string | number = overrides[0] ?? 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < characters.length; i++) {
        const char = characters[i];
        const styleKey: string | number = overrides[i] ?? 0;

        if (styleKey === currentStyleKey) {
          currentRunText += char;
        } else {
          if (currentRunText) {
            runs.push(
              this.createRun(
                currentRunText,
                currentStyleKey,
                styleTable,
                defaultFontFamily,
                defaultFontSize,
