          const readU32 = (off: number) =>
            (((d[off] || 0) << 24) >>> 0) +
            ((d[off + 1] || 0) << 16) +
            ((d[off + 2] || 0) << 8) +
            (d[off + 3] || 0);

          if (d.length >= 24 && d[0] === 0x89 && d[1] === 0x50 && d[2] === 0x4e && d[3] === 0x47) {
            imgW = readU32(16);
            imgH = readU32(20);
          } else if (d.length >= 4 && d[0] === 0xff && d[1] === 0xd8) {
            let offset = 2;
            while (offset < d.length - 8) {
              const marker = readU16(offset);
              offset += 2;
              if (marker === 0xffc0 || marker === 0xffc2) {
                imgH = readU16(offset + 3);
                imgW = readU16(offset + 5);
                break;
              }
              const len = readU16(offset);
              offset += len;
            }
          }
        }

        let picX = xHwpu;
        let picY = yHwpu;
        let picW = wHwpu;
        let picH = hHwpu;
        let dimW = imgW > 0 ? Math.round(imgW * 75) : wHwpu;
        let dimH = imgH > 0 ? Math.round(imgH * 75) : hHwpu;
        let clipLeft = 0;
        let clipRight = dimW;
        let clipTop = 0;
        let clipBottom = dimH;

        if (
          elem.imageCrop &&
          (elem.imageCrop.top > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.bottom > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.left > 0 ||
            elem.imageCrop.right > 0)
        ) {
          const cLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.left));
          const cRight = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.right));
          const cTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.top));
          const cBottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.95, elem.imageCrop.bottom));
          clipLeft = Math.round(dimW * cLeft);
          clipRight = Math.round(dimW * (1 - cRight));
          clipTop = Math.round(dimH * cTop);
          clipBottom = Math.round(dimH * (1 - cBottom));
        } else if (elem.imageScaleMode === "FIT" && imgW > 0 && imgH > 0) {
          const arImg = imgW / imgH;
          const arBox = wHwpu / hHwpu;
          if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
            picH = Math.max(100, Math.round(wHwpu / arImg));
            picY = Math.round(yHwpu + (hHwpu - picH) / 2);
          } else if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
            picW = Math.max(100, Math.round(hHwpu * arImg));
            picX = Math.round(xHwpu + (wHwpu - picW) / 2);
          }
          clipLeft = 0;
          clipRight = dimW;
          clipTop = 0;
          clipBottom = dimH;
        } else if (imgW > 0 && imgH > 0) {
          const arImg = imgW / imgH;
          const arBox = wHwpu / hHwpu;

          if (arImg > arBox * 1.01) {
            const visW = dimH * arBox;
            const cropX = (dimW - visW) / 2.0;
            clipLeft = Math.round(cropX);
            clipRight = Math.round(dimW - cropX);
            clipTop = 0;
            clipBottom = dimH;
          } else if (arImg < arBox * 0.99) {
            const visH = dimW / arBox;
            const cropY = (dimH - visH) / 2.0;
            clipLeft = 0;
            clipRight = dimW;
            clipTop = Math.round(cropY);
            clipBottom = Math.round(dimH - cropY);
          } else {
            clipLeft = 0;
            clipRight = dimW;
            clipTop = 0;
            clipBottom = dimH;
          }
        }

        const picId = nextId();
        return [
          this.renderPicXml({
            id: picId,
            zOrder,
            x: picX,
            y: picY,
            w: picW,
            h: picH,
            binId,
            clipLeft,
            clipRight,
            clipTop,
            clipBottom,
            dimW,
            dimH,
          }),
        ];
      }
    }

    // 2. Line (Canonical representation: thin filled rect for H/V dividers matching authentic Hancom, or native hp:line)
    if (elem.type === "line") {
      const isHorizontal = elem.bounds.height <= 4 || elem.bounds.width >= elem.bounds.height * 2;
      const isVertical = elem.bounds.width <= 4 || elem.bounds.height >= elem.bounds.width * 2;
      const isSolid = !elem.style.strokeDashed;

      // Authentic Hancom standard: 100% of horizontal/vertical dividers are serialized as thin filled rectangles
      if ((isHorizontal || isVertical) && isSolid) {
        const rectId = nextId();
        const strokeColor = cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000");
        const strokeWidthPt = elem.style.strokeWidth || 1;
        const strokeHwpu = Math.max(75, Math.round(strokeWidthPt * 100));

        const lineW = isHorizontal ? wHwpu : strokeHwpu;
        const lineH = isHorizontal ? strokeHwpu : hHwpu;

        return [
          this.renderRectXml({
            id: rectId,
            zOrder,
            x: xHwpu,
            y: yHwpu,
            w: lineW,
            h: lineH,
            fillColorHex: strokeColor,
            lineColorHex: "000000",
            lineWidth: "0",
            lineStyle: "NONE",
            ratio: 0,
          }),
        ];
      }

      // Otherwise render as native KS X 6101 / OWPML <hp:line> with valid startPt/endPt and isReverseHV="0"
      const lineId = nextId();
      const strokeColor = cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000");
      const strokeW = Math.max(10, Math.round((elem.style.strokeWidth || 1) * 10)).toString();
      const strokeStyle = elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID";
      const lineShapeW = Math.max(1, wHwpu);
      const lineShapeH = Math.max(0, hHwpu);
      return [
        this.renderLineXml({
          id: lineId,
          zOrder,
          x: xHwpu,
          y: yHwpu,
          w: lineShapeW,
          h: lineShapeH,
          startPt: { x: 0, y: 0 },
          endPt: { x: lineShapeW, y: lineShapeH },
          colorHex: strokeColor,
          width: strokeW,
          style: strokeStyle,
          endCap: "FLAT",
        }),
      ];
    }

    // Common text & style calculation
    const isText = elem.type === "text" || (elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0);
    const hasFill = !!elem.style.fillColor;
    const hasStroke = !!elem.style.strokeColor && (elem.style.strokeWidth || 0) > 0;

    const lineStyle = hasStroke ? (elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID") : "NONE";
    const lineColor = hasStroke ? cleanHex(elem.style.strokeColor, "000000") : "000000";
    const lineWidth = hasStroke ? Math.round((elem.style.strokeWidth || 1) * 10).toString() : "0";

    const fillBrushXml = hasFill
      ? `<hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="#${cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor)}" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>`
      : `<hc:fillBrush/>`;

    const shapeW = wHwpu;
    const shapeH = hHwpu;

    let drawTextPart = "";
    if (isText && elem.textRuns && elem.textRuns.length > 0) {
      const firstLineHeightPt = elem.textRuns.find((r) => r.lineHeightPt && r.lineHeightPt > 0)?.lineHeightPt;
      const paraPrId = getParaPrId(elem.paragraphAlign, firstLineHeightPt);

      // Split runs across lines into separate paragraphs
      const paragraphs: Array<Array<{ charPrId: number; text: string }>> = [[]];

      for (const run of elem.textRuns) {
        const charPrId = getCharPrId(run);
        const lines = run.text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
          if (i > 0) {
            paragraphs.push([]);
          }
          if (lines[i].length > 0 || lines.length === 1) {
            paragraphs[paragraphs.length - 1].push({ charPrId, text: lines[i] });
          }
        }
      }

      const parasXml: string[] = paragraphs.map((paraRuns, pIdx) => {
        const runsStr =
          paraRuns.length > 0
            ? paraRuns
                .map((r) => `<hp:run charPrIDRef="${r.charPrId}"><hp:t>${escapeXml(r.text)}</hp:t></hp:run>`)
                .join("")
            : `<hp:run charPrIDRef="0"><hp:t/></hp:run>`;
        return `<hp:p id="${pIdx}" paraPrIDRef="${paraPrId}" styleIDRef="0" pageBreak="0" columnBreak="0" merged="0">${runsStr}</hp:p>`;
      });

      const drawName = escapeXml(elem.name || elem.id || "");
      const cornerRadiusPt = elem.style?.cornerRadius || 0;
      const isPillOrBadge =
        (elem.bounds.height <= 36 || cornerRadiusPt >= elem.bounds.height / 3) && paragraphs.length <= 1;

      const vertAlignAttr =
        elem.verticalAlign === "center" || (hasFill && !elem.verticalAlign && isPillOrBadge)
          ? "CENTER"
          : elem.verticalAlign === "bottom"
          ? "BOTTOM"
          : "TOP";

      // If shape has rounded corners and no explicit padding, provide safe internal margin to avoid clipping
      const defaultPadL = hasFill && cornerRadiusPt > 0 ? Math.round(cornerRadiusPt * 0.4) : 0;
      const defaultPadT = hasFill && cornerRadiusPt > 0 && vertAlignAttr !== "CENTER" ? Math.round(cornerRadiusPt * 0.3) : 0;

      const marginL = elem.innerPaddingPt
        ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.left ?? defaultPadL)
        : ptToHwpu(defaultPadL);
      const marginR = elem.innerPaddingPt
        ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.right ?? defaultPadL)
        : ptToHwpu(defaultPadL);
      const marginT = elem.innerPaddingPt
        ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.top ?? defaultPadT)
        : ptToHwpu(defaultPadT);
      const marginB = elem.innerPaddingPt
        ? ptToHwpu(elem.innerPaddingPt.bottom ?? defaultPadT)
        : ptToHwpu(defaultPadT);

      drawTextPart = `
  <hp:drawText lastWidth="${shapeW}" name="${drawName}" editable="1">
    <hp:subList id="" textDirection="HORIZONTAL" lineWrap="BREAK" vertAlign="${vertAlignAttr}" linkListIDRef="0" linkListNextIDRef="0" textWidth="${shapeW}" textHeight="0" hasTextRef="0" hasNumRef="0">
      ${parasXml.join("\n      ")}
    </hp:subList>
    <hp:textMargin left="${marginL}" right="${marginR}" top="${marginT}" bottom="${marginB}"/>
  </hp:drawText>`;
    }

    // 3. Vector Shape (KS X 6101 native hp:polygon for closed paths / hp:line for open subpaths)
    if (elem.type === "vector") {
      let subpaths = elem.vectorSubpaths;
      if (!subpaths || subpaths.length === 0) {
        if (elem.rawSvgPath) {
          subpaths = SvgPath.parse(elem.rawSvgPath).toHwpxSubpathsWithClosed(shapeW, shapeH);
        } else if (elem.vectorPoints && elem.vectorPoints.length > 0) {
          subpaths = [{ points: elem.vectorPoints, closed: true }];
        }
      }

      if (subpaths && subpaths.length > 0) {
        const renderedShapes: string[] = [];
        for (let idx = 0; idx < subpaths.length; idx++) {
          const sp = subpaths[idx];
          const pts = sp.points;

          // Open subpath: emit connected <hp:line> segments
          if (!sp.closed && pts.length >= 2) {
            const openStrokeColor = hasStroke
              ? lineColor
              : hasFill
              ? cleanHex(elem.style.fillColor, "000000")
              : "000000";
            const openStrokeWidth = hasStroke ? lineWidth : "15";
            const openStrokeStyle = elem.style.strokeDashed ? "DASH" : "SOLID";
            const endCap = pts.length > 2 ? "ROUND" : "FLAT";

            for (let segIdx = 0; segIdx < pts.length - 1; segIdx++) {
              const p0 = pts[segIdx];
              const p1 = pts[segIdx + 1];
              const lineId = nextId();
              renderedShapes.push(
                this.renderLineXml({
                  id: lineId,
                  zOrder,
                  x: xHwpu,
                  y: yHwpu,
                  w: shapeW,
                  h: shapeH,
                  p0: { x: p0.x, y: p0.y },
                  p1: { x: p1.x, y: p1.y },
                  colorHex: openStrokeColor,
                  width: openStrokeWidth,
                  style: openStrokeStyle,
                  endCap,
                })
              );
            }
            continue;
          }

          // Closed polygon
          const polyId = nextId();
          const subFillBrush =
            sp.closed && hasFill
              ? fillBrushXml
              : `<hc:fillBrush/>`;

          const polyPts =
            pts.length > 0
              ? pts
              : [
                  { x: 0, y: 0 },
                  { x: shapeW, y: 0 },
                  { x: shapeW, y: shapeH },
                  { x: 0, y: shapeH },
                ];

          renderedShapes.push(
            this.renderPolygonXml({
              id: polyId,
              zOrder,
              x: xHwpu,
              y: yHwpu,
              w: shapeW,
              h: shapeH,
              points: polyPts,
              colorHex: lineColor,
              width: lineWidth,
              style: lineStyle,
              fillBrushXml: subFillBrush,
              drawTextXml: idx === 0 ? drawTextPart : "",
            })
          );
        }

        return renderedShapes;
      }
    }

    // 4. Oval / Ellipse
    if (elem.type === "oval") {
      const ovalId = nextId();
      return [
        this.renderOvalXml({
          id: ovalId,
          zOrder,
          x: xHwpu,
          y: yHwpu,
          w: shapeW,
          h: shapeH,
          colorHex: lineColor,
          width: lineWidth,
          style: lineStyle,
          fillBrushXml,
          drawTextXml: drawTextPart,
        }),
      ];
    }

    // 5. Card / Rect / Textbox
    const cornerRadius = elem.style?.cornerRadius || 0;
    const minDim = Math.min(elem.bounds.width, elem.bounds.height);
    const ratio =
      cornerRadius > 0 && minDim > 0
        ? Math.min(50, Math.max(0, Math.round((cornerRadius / (minDim / 2)) * 50)))
        : 0;

    const rectId = nextId();
    return [
      this.renderRectXml({
        id: rectId,
        zOrder,
        x: xHwpu,
        y: yHwpu,
        w: shapeW,
        h: shapeH,
        lineColorHex: lineColor,
        lineWidth: lineWidth,
        lineStyle: lineStyle,
        colorHex: lineColor,
        width: lineWidth,
        style: lineStyle,
        fillBrushXml,
        drawTextXml: drawTextPart,
        ratio,
      }),
    ];
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:rect> with strict KS X 6101 element sequence and coordinates
   */
  private renderRectXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    fillColorHex?: string;
    lineColorHex?: string;
    lineWidth?: string;
    lineStyle?: string;
    colorHex?: string;
    width?: string;
    style?: string;
    ratio: number;
    fillBrushXml?: string;
    drawTextXml?: string;
  }): string {
    const rawColor = p.lineColorHex || p.colorHex || "000000";
    const rawWidth = p.lineWidth || p.width || "0";
    const rawStyle = p.lineStyle || p.style || "NONE";

    const cleanLineColor = cleanHex(rawColor, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(rawWidth).trim()) ? String(rawWidth).trim() : "0";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(rawStyle)
      ? rawStyle
      : "NONE";

    const fillBrush =
      p.fillBrushXml ||
      (p.fillColorHex
        ? `<hc:fillBrush><hc:winBrush faceColor="#${cleanHex(p.fillColorHex)}" hatchColor="#999999" alpha="0"/></hc:fillBrush>`
        : `<hc:fillBrush/>`);

    const drawText = p.drawTextXml || "";

    return `<hp:rect id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}" ratio="${p.ratio}">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${cleanLineColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${fillBrush}${drawText}
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hc:pt0 x="0" y="0"/>
  <hc:pt1 x="${p.w}" y="0"/>
  <hc:pt2 x="${p.w}" y="${p.h}"/>
  <hc:pt3 x="0" y="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:rect>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:ellipse> conforming to KS X 6101 and authentic Hancom documents
   */
  private renderOvalXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    colorHex: string;
    width: string;
    style: string;
    fillBrushXml: string;
    drawTextXml: string;
  }): string {
    const cx = Math.round(p.w / 2);
    const cy = Math.round(p.h / 2);

    const safeColor = cleanHex(p.colorHex, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(p.width).trim()) ? String(p.width).trim() : "0";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(p.style)
      ? p.style
      : "NONE";

    return `<hp:ellipse intervalDirty="0" hasArcPr="0" arcType="NORMAL" id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${cx}" centerY="${cy}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${safeColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${p.fillBrushXml}${p.drawTextXml}
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hc:center x="${cx}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:ax1 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:ax2 x="${cx}" y="${p.h}"/>
  <hc:start1 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:end1 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:start2 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hc:end2 x="${p.w}" y="${cy}"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:ellipse>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:line> with hc:startPt, hc:endPt conforming to OWPML LineType
   */
  private renderLineXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    startPt?: { x: number; y: number };
    endPt?: { x: number; y: number };
    p0?: { x: number; y: number };
    p1?: { x: number; y: number };
    colorHex: string;
    width: string;
    style: string;
    endCap: string;
  }): string {
    const safeColor = cleanHex(p.colorHex, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(p.width).trim()) ? String(p.width).trim() : "10";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(p.style)
      ? p.style
      : "SOLID";

    const startX = p.startPt?.x ?? p.p0?.x ?? 0;
    const startY = p.startPt?.y ?? p.p0?.y ?? 0;
    const endX = p.endPt?.x ?? p.p1?.x ?? p.w;
    const endY = p.endPt?.y ?? p.p1?.y ?? p.h;

    return `<hp:line id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}" isReverseHV="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${safeColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="${p.endCap}" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hc:startPt x="${startX}" y="${startY}"/>
  <hc:endPt x="${endX}" y="${endY}"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:line>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:polygon> with hc:pt list conforming to OWPML PolygonType
   */
  private renderPolygonXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    points: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>;
    colorHex: string;
    width: string;
    style: string;
    fillBrushXml: string;
    drawTextXml: string;
  }): string {
    const safeColor = cleanHex(p.colorHex, "000000");
    const safeWidth = /^\d+$/.test(String(p.width).trim()) ? String(p.width).trim() : "0";
    const safeStyle = ["NONE", "SOLID", "DASH", "DOT", "DASH_DOT", "DASH_DOT_DOT"].includes(p.style)
      ? p.style
      : "NONE";

    const closedPts = [...p.points];
    if (closedPts.length >= 3) {
      const first = closedPts[0];
      const last = closedPts[closedPts.length - 1];
      if (first.x !== last.x || first.y !== last.y) {
        closedPts.push({ x: first.x, y: first.y });
      }
    }

    const ptXml = closedPts.map((pt) => `<hc:pt x="${pt.x}" y="${pt.y}"/>`).join("\n  ");

    return `<hp:polygon id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="NONE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#${safeColor}" width="${safeWidth}" style="${safeStyle}" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  ${p.fillBrushXml}${p.drawTextXml}
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  ${ptXml}
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:polygon>`;
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Serializes <hp:pic> with nested imgRect points, imgClip, inMargin, imgDim, and hc:img
   */
  private renderPicXml(p: {
    id: number;
    zOrder: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    binId: string;
    clipLeft: number;
    clipRight: number;
    clipTop: number;
    clipBottom: number;
    dimW: number;
    dimH: number;
  }): string {
    return `<hp:pic id="${p.id}" zOrder="${p.zOrder}" numberingType="PICTURE" textWrap="IN_FRONT_OF_TEXT" textFlow="BOTH_SIDES" lock="0" dropcapstyle="None" href="" groupLevel="0" instid="${p.id}" reverse="0">
  <hp:offset x="0" y="0"/>
  <hp:orgSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:curSz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}"/>
  <hp:flip horizontal="0" vertical="0"/>
  <hp:rotationInfo angle="0" centerX="${Math.round(p.w / 2)}" centerY="${Math.round(p.h / 2)}" rotateimage="1"/>
  <hp:renderingInfo>
    <hc:transMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:scaMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
    <hc:rotMatrix e1="1" e2="0" e3="0" e4="0" e5="1" e6="0"/>
  </hp:renderingInfo>
  <hp:lineShape color="#000000" width="0" style="NONE" endCap="FLAT" headStyle="NORMAL" tailStyle="NORMAL" headfill="0" tailfill="0" headSz="SMALL_SMALL" tailSz="SMALL_SMALL" outlineStyle="NORMAL" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:imgRect>
    <hc:pt0 x="0" y="0"/>
    <hc:pt1 x="${p.w}" y="0"/>
    <hc:pt2 x="${p.w}" y="${p.h}"/>
    <hc:pt3 x="0" y="${p.h}"/>
  </hp:imgRect>
  <hp:imgClip left="${p.clipLeft}" right="${p.clipRight}" top="${p.clipTop}" bottom="${p.clipBottom}"/>
  <hp:inMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
  <hp:imgDim dimwidth="${p.dimW}" dimheight="${p.dimH}"/>
  <hc:img binaryItemIDRef="${p.binId}" bright="0" contrast="0" effect="REAL_PIC" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:effects/>
  <hp:shadow type="NONE" color="#B2B2B2" offsetX="0" offsetY="0" alpha="0"/>
  <hp:sz width="${p.w}" height="${p.h}" widthRelTo="ABSOLUTE" heightRelTo="ABSOLUTE" protect="0"/>
  <hp:pos treatAsChar="0" affectLSpacing="0" flowWithText="0" allowOverlap="1" holdAnchorAndSO="1" vertRelTo="PAPER" horzRelTo="PAPER" vertAlign="TOP" horzAlign="LEFT" vertOffset="${p.y}" horzOffset="${p.x}"/>
  <hp:outMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>
</hp:pic>`;
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: tests/hwpx-schema-conformance.test.ts
================================================================================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 20: HWPX OWPML / KS X 6101 Schema Conformance & Recovery Suite", () => {
  it("strictly wraps hp:secPr inside hp:run charPrIDRef=0 and follows exact secPr child sequence", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "SecPr Structure Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "t1",
              type: "text",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 30 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              textRuns: [{ text: "섹션 구조 검증", fontSizePt: 12 }],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // 1. hp:secPr MUST be inside hp:run inside the first hp:p
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:p\b[^>]*>\s*<hp:run charPrIDRef="0">\s*<hp:secPr\b/);

    // 2. hp:ctrl with hp:colPr must follow hp:secPr inside the same run
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<\/hp:secPr>\s*<hp:ctrl>\s*<hp:colPr\b/);

    // 3. Child sequence inside hp:secPr:
    // grid -> startNum -> visibility -> lineNumberShape -> pagePr -> footNotePr -> endNotePr -> pageBorderFill
    const gridIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:grid");
    const startNumIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:startNum");
    const visIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:visibility");
    const lineNumIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:lineNumberShape");
    const pagePrIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pagePr");
    const footIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:footNotePr");
    const endIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:endNotePr");
    const borderFillIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pageBorderFill");

    expect(gridIdx).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(startNumIdx).toBeGreaterThan(gridIdx);
    expect(visIdx).toBeGreaterThan(startNumIdx);
    expect(lineNumIdx).toBeGreaterThan(visIdx);
    expect(pagePrIdx).toBeGreaterThan(lineNumIdx);
    expect(footIdx).toBeGreaterThan(pagePrIdx);
    expect(endIdx).toBeGreaterThan(footIdx);
    expect(borderFillIdx).toBeGreaterThan(endIdx);
  });

  it("strictly enforces child element order and unsignedInt ID for shapes", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Shape Order Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "rect1",
              type: "card",
              bounds: { x: 20, y: 20, width: 200, height: 100 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: { fillColor: "#EEEEEE", strokeColor: "#333333", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 8 },
              textRuns: [{ text: "도형 순서 검증", fontSizePt: 10 }],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // Shape ID and instid must be pure integers, NOT string paths like "1_1"
    const idMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:rect id="(\d+)"[^>]*instid="(\d+)"/);
    expect(idMatch).toBeDefined();
    expect(idMatch![1]).toBe(idMatch![2]);

    // Sequence inside hp:rect:
    // offset -> orgSz -> curSz -> flip -> rotationInfo -> renderingInfo -> lineShape -> fillBrush -> drawText -> shadow -> geometry (hc:pt0..3) -> sz -> pos -> outMargin
    const offsetIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:offset");
    const orgSzIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:orgSz");
    const curSzIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:curSz");
    const flipIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:flip");
    const rotIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:rotationInfo");
    const rendIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:renderingInfo");
    const lineShapeIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:lineShape");
    const fillBrushIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:fillBrush");
    const drawTextIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:drawText");
    const shadowIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:shadow");
    const pt0Idx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:pt0");
    const pt3Idx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:pt3");
    const szIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:sz");
    const posIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pos");
    const outMarginIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:outMargin");

    expect(offsetIdx).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(orgSzIdx).toBeGreaterThan(offsetIdx);
    expect(curSzIdx).toBeGreaterThan(orgSzIdx);
    expect(flipIdx).toBeGreaterThan(curSzIdx);
    expect(rotIdx).toBeGreaterThan(flipIdx);
    expect(rendIdx).toBeGreaterThan(rotIdx);
    expect(lineShapeIdx).toBeGreaterThan(rendIdx);
    expect(fillBrushIdx).toBeGreaterThan(lineShapeIdx);
    expect(drawTextIdx).toBeGreaterThan(fillBrushIdx);
    expect(shadowIdx).toBeGreaterThan(drawTextIdx);
    expect(pt0Idx).toBeGreaterThan(shadowIdx);
    expect(pt3Idx).toBeGreaterThan(pt0Idx);
    expect(szIdx).toBeGreaterThan(pt3Idx);
    expect(posIdx).toBeGreaterThan(szIdx);
    expect(outMarginIdx).toBeGreaterThan(posIdx);
  });

  it("renders dedicated geometry for hp:ellipse conforming to KS X 6101 ellipse specification", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Oval Geometry Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "oval1",
              type: "oval",
              bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 80 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: { fillColor: "#00AAEE", strokeColor: "#003366", strokeWidth: 2 },
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // hp:ellipse tag and attributes (conforming to KS X 6101 and authentic Hancom corpus)
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:ellipse");
    expect(secXml).toContain('intervalDirty="0"');
    expect(secXml).toContain('hasArcPr="0"');
    expect(secXml).toContain('arcType="NORMAL"');

    // Dedicated oval geometry tags
    // W = 10000 HWPU, H = 8000 HWPU
    // center: (5000, 4000)
    // ax1: (10000, 4000)
    // ax2: (5000, 8000)
    // start1..end2: (10000, 4000)
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:center x="5000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:ax1 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:ax2 x="5000" y="8000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:start1 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:end1 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:start2 x="10000" y="4000"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:end2 x="10000" y="4000"/>');

    // Must also contain shadow, sz, pos, outMargin in proper sequence
    const centerIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hc:center");
    const szIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:sz", centerIdx);
    const posIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:pos", szIdx);
    const outMarginIdx = secXml.indexOf("<hp:outMargin", posIdx);

    expect(centerIdx).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(szIdx).toBeGreaterThan(centerIdx);
    expect(posIdx).toBeGreaterThan(szIdx);
    expect(outMarginIdx).toBeGreaterThan(posIdx);
  });

  it("renders hp:pic with nested imgRect points, imgClip, inMargin, and single binaryItemIDRef", async () => {
    const pngHeader = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d, 0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0x00, // width: 256
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x80, // height: 128
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Image Structure Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [
            {
              id: "img1",
              type: "image",
              bounds: { x: 10, y: 10, width: 200, height: 100 },
              zIndex: 1,
              style: {},
              imageRef: "res_photo",
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
      resources: {
        res_photo: {
          mimeType: "image/png",
          data: pngHeader,
        },
      },
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

    // 1. Tag attributes
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:pic id="\d+" zOrder="1" numberingType="PICTURE" [^>]*reverse="0">/);

    // 2. imgRect with nested hc:pt0..3
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:imgRect>");
    expect(secXml).toMatch(/<hp:imgRect>\s*<hc:pt0 x="0" y="0"\/>\s*<hc:pt1\b/);

    // 3. Spurious inImgDim and binRef must NOT exist
    expect(secXml).not.toContain("<hp:inImgDim");
    expect(secXml).not.toContain('binRef=');

    // 4. Proper tags: imgClip, inMargin, imgDim, hc:img, effects, shadow, sz, pos, outMargin
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:imgClip");
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hp:inMargin left="0" right="0" top="0" bottom="0"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:imgDim");
    expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:img binaryItemIDRef="BIN0001" bright="0" contrast="0" effect="REAL_PIC" alpha="0"/>');
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:effects/>");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:shadow");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:sz");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:pos");
    expect(secXml).toContain("<hp:outMargin");
  });

  it("renders uppercase fontface lang codes in Contents/header.xml", async () => {
    const doc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Header Lang Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        {
          pageNumber: 1,
          bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
          elements: [],
        },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");

    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="HANGUL"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="LATIN"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="HANJA"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="JAPANESE"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="OTHER"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="SYMBOL"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('lang="USER"');

    // Ensure lowercase was completely eliminated
    expect(headerXml).not.toContain('lang="hangul"');
    expect(headerXml).not.toContain('lang="latin"');
  });

  it("renders complete mandatory children in default charPr id=0 when charPrList is empty", async () => {
    const emptyDoc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Empty CharPr Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [{ pageNumber: 1, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] }],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(emptyDoc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");

    // Must contain complete child set conforming to OWPML charPr schema
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:charPr id="0"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:fontRef hangul="0"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:ratio hangul="100"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:spacing hangul="0"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:relSz hangul="100"');
    expect(headerXml).toContain('<hh:offset hangul="0"');
  });

  it("strictly sets secCnt='1' in header.xml for single-section multi-page documents", async () => {
    const multiPageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
      title: "Multi Page Section Count Test",
      pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
      marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
      pages: [
        { pageNumber: 1, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] },
        { pageNumber: 2, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] },
        { pageNumber: 3, bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 }, elements: [] },
      ],
      resources: {},
      diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
    };

    const renderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
    const buf = await renderer.renderToBuffer(multiPageDoc);
    const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(buf);
    const headerXml = await zip.file("Contents/header.xml")!.async("string");

    // secCnt must match actual section files in package (1), not page count
    expect(headerXml).toMatch(/<hh:head\b[^>]*secCnt="1"/);
  });
});



================================================================================
FILE: tests/phase16-1-remediation.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import JSZip from "jszip";
import { FigmaCanvasParser } from "../src/canvas/parser/figmaCanvasParser.js";
import { DocxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/docxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { HwpxCanvasRenderer } from "../src/canvas/renderer/hwpxCanvasRenderer.js";
import { CanvasDocument, CanvasElement } from "../src/canvas/types.js";

describe("Phase 16.1 Independent Audit Findings Remediation Tests", () => {
  // =========================================================================
  // 1. P0: Real Figma Input Landscape Handling (complex-nested-dashboard)
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P0: Real Figma Input Landscape Handling", () => {
    it("should dynamically detect landscape orientation and dimensions from top-level Figma frame", async () => {
      const samplePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/synthetic-figma/complex-nested-dashboard.json");
      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(samplePath, "utf-8"));

      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Dashboard Landscape" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      // Frame is 842 x 595 (A4 landscape ratio)
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeGreaterThan(doc.pageSize.heightPt);
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.orientation).toBe("landscape");

      // Verify DOCX Output has landscape orientation and matching twip dimensions
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");

      const pgSzMatch = docXml.match(/<w:pgSz\b[^>]*\/?>/);
      expect(pgSzMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:orient="landscape"');
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:w="16838"');
      expect(pgSzMatch![0]).toContain('w:h="11906"');

      // Verify HWPX Output has WIDELY and matching HWPU dimensions
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const pagePrMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:pagePr\b[^>]*\/?>/);
      expect(pagePrMatch).toBeDefined();
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('landscape="WIDELY"');
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('width="84189"');
      expect(pagePrMatch![0]).toContain('height="59528"');
    });

    it("should preserve portrait A4 dimensions for portrait documents (multi-page-report)", () => {
      const samplePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "samples/synthetic-figma/multi-page-report.json");
      const rawJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(samplePath, "utf-8"));

      const parser = new FigmaCanvasParser({ titleOverride: "Multi-page Portrait" });
      const doc = parser.parse(rawJson);

      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeGreaterThan(doc.pageSize.widthPt);
      expect(doc.pageSize.widthPt).toBeCloseTo(595.28, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.heightPt).toBeCloseTo(841.89, 0);
      expect(doc.pageSize.orientation).toBe("portrait");
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. P1: HWPX Multi-Subpath and Open Path Separation
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: HWPX Multi-Subpath and Open Path", () => {
    it("should emit multiple disconnected subpaths as separate HWPX polygons", async () => {
      const compoundDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Compound Subpath Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "vec1",
                name: "Two Boxes",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#00FF00", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1 },
                rawSvgPath: "M 0 0 L 50 0 L 50 50 L 0 50 Z M 100 0 L 150 0 L 150 50 L 100 50 Z",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(compoundDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const polygonMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:polygon\b/g);
      expect(polygonMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(polygonMatches!.length).toBe(2);
    });

    it("should preserve open paths (like checkmarks) without closing lines or spurious fills", async () => {
      const checkmarkDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Checkmark Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "check1",
                name: "Checkmark",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { strokeColor: "#0000FF", strokeWidth: 2 },
                rawSvgPath: "M 10 50 L 40 80 L 90 20",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(checkmarkDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Open paths must NOT be emitted as <hp:polygon> (which auto-closes in Hancom)
      expect(secXml).not.toContain("<hp:polygon");
      const lineMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b/g);
      expect(lineMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatches!.length).toBe(2);
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:startPt x="0" y="5000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:endPt x="3750" y="10000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:endPt x="10000" y="0"/>');
    });

    it("should distinguish closed vs open subpaths in mixed multi-contour paths", async () => {
      const mixedDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Mixed Path Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "mix1",
                name: "Circle and Line",
                type: "vector",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 200 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#FF0000", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1 },
                // Closed outer box + open inner slash
                rawSvgPath: "M 0 0 L 100 0 L 100 100 L 0 100 Z M 20 20 L 80 80",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(mixedDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      // Closed box is emitted as <hp:polygon> with fill; open slash is emitted as <hp:line> without fill
      const polygonMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:polygon\b/g);
      expect(polygonMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(polygonMatches!.length).toBe(1);

      const lineMatches = secXml.match(/<hp:line\b/g);
      expect(lineMatches).toBeDefined();
      expect(lineMatches!.length).toBe(1);

      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:winBrush faceColor="#FF0000"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:startPt x="4000" y="4000"/>');
      expect(secXml).toContain('<hc:endPt x="16000" y="16000"/>');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. P1: Image FILL Crop Verification
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: Image FILL Crop Verification", () => {
    const widePngBytes = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d,
      0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // width = 200
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // height = 100
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    const tallPngBytes = new Uint8Array([
      0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x0d,
      0x49, 0x48, 0x44, 0x52,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x64, // width = 100
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xc8, // height = 200
      0x08, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
      0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    ]);

    it("should calculate correct imgClip bounds for horizontal overflow in HWPX (left & right crop)", async () => {
      const imageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Crop Test Wide",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img1",
                name: "Wide Image in Square Box",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "sample_wide.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "sample_wide.png": {
            id: "sample_wide.png",
            name: "sample_wide.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: widePngBytes,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imageDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const imgClipMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:imgClip\s+([^>]+)\/>/);
      expect(imgClipMatch).toBeDefined();
      const clipAttrs = imgClipMatch![1];

      const leftVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/left="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      const rightVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/right="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      expect(leftVal).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(rightVal).toBeLessThan(20000);
    });

    it("should calculate correct imgClip bounds for vertical overflow in HWPX (top & bottom crop)", async () => {
      const imageDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Crop Test Tall",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "img2",
                name: "Tall Image in Square Box",
                type: "image",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: {},
                imageRef: "sample_tall.png",
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {
          "sample_tall.png": {
            id: "sample_tall.png",
            name: "sample_tall.png",
            mimeType: "image/png",
            data: tallPngBytes,
          },
        },
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(imageDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const imgClipMatch = secXml.match(/<hp:imgClip\s+([^>]+)\/>/);
      expect(imgClipMatch).toBeDefined();
      const clipAttrs = imgClipMatch![1];

      const topVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/top="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      const bottomVal = parseInt(clipAttrs.match(/bottom="(\d+)"/)![1], 10);
      expect(topVal).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(bottomVal).toBeLessThan(20000);
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 4. P1: Text Vertical Alignment F-12
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P1: Text Vertical Alignment F-12", () => {
    it("should preserve TOP, CENTER, BOTTOM alignment across Canvas IR, DOCX, and HWPX", async () => {
      const figmaParser = new FigmaCanvasParser();
      const textTop = {
        id: "text:top",
        name: "Top Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Top Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "TOP" },
      };
      const textCenter = {
        id: "text:center",
        name: "Center Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Center Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 150, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "CENTER" },
      };
      const textBottom = {
        id: "text:bottom",
        name: "Bottom Text",
        type: "TEXT",
        characters: "Bottom Aligned",
        absoluteBoundingBox: { x: 50, y: 250, width: 200, height: 80 },
        style: { fontSize: 14, fontFamily: "Calibri", textAlignVertical: "BOTTOM" },
      };

      const doc = figmaParser.parse({
        id: "0:0",
        name: "Vertical Align Comparison",
        type: "DOCUMENT",
        children: [textTop, textCenter, textBottom],
      } as any);

      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[0].verticalAlign).toBe("top");
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[1].verticalAlign).toBe("center");
      expect(doc.pages[0].elements[2].verticalAlign).toBe("bottom");

      // Verify DOCX VML emits exact v-text-anchor tags
      const docxRenderer = new DocxCanvasRenderer();
      const docxBuf = await docxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const docxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(docxBuf);
      const docXml = await docxZip.file("word/document.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:top");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:middle");
      expect(docXml).toContain("v-text-anchor:bottom");

      // Verify HWPX emits exact vertAlign attributes
      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(doc);
      const hwpxZip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await hwpxZip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="TOP"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="CENTER"');
      expect(secXml).toContain('vertAlign="BOTTOM"');
    });
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // 5. P2: HWPX Rounded Corners F-13
  // =========================================================================
  describe("P2: HWPX Rounded Corners F-13", () => {
    it("should compute accurate ratio for cornerRadius=0, 8, 24 and cards with stroke & fill", async () => {
      const cardsDoc: CanvasDocument = {
        title: "Corner Radius Scale Test",
        pageSize: { name: "A4", widthPt: 595.28, heightPt: 841.89, unit: "pt" },
        marginsPt: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        pages: [
          {
            pageNumber: 1,
            bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 595.28, height: 841.89 },
            elements: [
              {
                id: "c0",
                name: "Sharp Rect (r=0)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 50, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 1,
                style: { fillColor: "#FFFFFF", cornerRadius: 0 },
              },
              {
                id: "c8",
                name: "Round Card (r=8)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 160, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 2,
                style: { fillColor: "#F0F0F0", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 8 },
              },
              {
                id: "c24",
                name: "Round Card (r=24)",
                type: "card",
                bounds: { x: 50, y: 270, width: 100, height: 100 },
                zIndex: 3,
                style: { fillColor: "#E0E0E0", strokeColor: "#000000", strokeWidth: 1, cornerRadius: 24 },
              },
            ],
          },
        ],
        resources: {},
        diagnostics: { notices: [], warnings: [] },
      };

      const hwpxRenderer = new HwpxCanvasRenderer();
      const hwpxBuf = await hwpxRenderer.renderToBuffer(cardsDoc);
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(hwpxBuf);
      const secXml = await zip.file("Contents/section0.xml")!.async("string");

      const rectMatches = Array.from(secXml.matchAll(/<hp:rect\b[^>]*\bratio="(\d+)"/g));
      expect(rectMatches.length).toBe(3);

      const ratio0 = parseInt(rectMatches[0][1], 10);
      const ratio8 = parseInt(rectMatches[1][1], 10);
      const ratio24 = parseInt(rectMatches[2][1], 10);

      expect(ratio0).toBe(0);
      expect(ratio8).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(ratio24).toBeGreaterThan(ratio8);
    });
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: tests/phase17-quality-integration.test.ts
